--- lines 333-344 ---
func _exit_tree() -> void:
    if upnp_thread != null:
        var result_variant: Variant = upnp_thread.wait_to_finish()
        upnp_thread = null
        if result_variant is Dictionary and bool((result_variant as Dictionary).get("ok", false)):
            var result_gateway: Variant = (result_variant as Dictionary).get("gateway")
            if result_gateway is UPNP:
                (result_gateway as UPNP).delete_port_mapping(PORT, "UDP")
                return
    if upnp_mapping_owned and upnp_gateway != null:
        upnp_gateway.delete_port_mapping(PORT, "UDP")


--- lines 529-547 ---
func _build_vehicle_drive_mask() -> void:
    downtown_vehicle_drive_mask.resize(256 * 256)
    downtown_vehicle_drive_mask.fill(0)
    var tile_size: float = DOWNTOWN_DATA.TILE_SIZE
    for raw_route: Variant in population_vehicle_routes:
        var route: Array[Vector3] = _route_from_variant(raw_route)
        if route.size() < 2:
            continue
        for segment_index in range(route.size() - 1):
            var start: Vector3 = route[segment_index]
            var finish: Vector3 = route[segment_index + 1]
            var distance: float = Vector2(finish.x - start.x, finish.z - start.z).length()
            var steps: int = maxi(1, ceili(distance / (tile_size * 0.45)))
            for step in range(steps + 1):
                var point: Vector3 = start.lerp(finish, float(step) / float(steps))
                var cell_x: int = clampi(floori(point.x / tile_size), 0, 255)
                var cell_y: int = clampi(floori(point.z / tile_size), 0, 255)
                downtown_vehicle_drive_mask[cell_y * 256 + cell_x] = 1


--- lines 577-592 ---
func is_vehicle_surface_allowed(from_position: Vector3, to_position: Vector3) -> bool:
    var surface_info: Vector2i = _surface_block_info(to_position, from_position.y - 0.10)
    var slope_type: int = surface_info.x
    if slope_type == 0:
        return true
    # GTA2 ground type 1 is an actual road. Accept its complete width, including
    # all eight cells of long inclines, instead of only the Green-Arrow lane
    # centre. This restores player steering freedom on sloped streets.
    if surface_info.y == 1:
        return true
    var tile_size: float = DOWNTOWN_DATA.TILE_SIZE
    var cell_x: int = clampi(floori(to_position.x / tile_size), 0, 255)
    var cell_y: int = clampi(floori(to_position.z / tile_size), 0, 255)
    var cell_index: int = cell_y * 256 + cell_x
    return cell_index >= 0 and cell_index < downtown_vehicle_drive_mask.size() and downtown_vehicle_drive_mask[cell_index] != 0


--- lines 754-804 ---
func _build_vehicles() -> void:
    # Parked / quest-adjacent cars stay persistent. Ambient street traffic comes
    # from a recycled pool, matching GTA2's camera-local population approach.
    # These are deliberate parking transforms, not random Green-Arrow nodes.
    # IDs 1/2/26/13 previously overlapped the traffic graph and could deadlock
    # an entire lane. Curb cars follow traffic direction; service vehicles use
    # the marked lot at Downtown x=516..526 / z=96.
    _create_vehicle(1, Vector3(351.25, 0.10, 323.75), PI * 0.5, "jefferson")
    _create_vehicle(2, Vector3(293.75, 0.10, 333.75), PI, "taxi")
    _create_vehicle(26, Vector3(351.25, 0.10, 308.75), PI * 0.5, "beamer")
    _create_vehicle(11, Vector3(81.25, 0.10, 525.00), PI, "sports_limousine")
    _create_vehicle(12, Vector3(516.25, 0.10, 96.25), -PI * 0.5, "tow_truck")
    _create_vehicle(13, Vector3(526.25, 0.10, 96.25), -PI * 0.5, "box_truck")
    # The marked service lot has seven clear parking cells and no traffic graph
    # crossing it. Five parked vehicles fill it without blocking the road.
    _create_vehicle(14, Vector3(511.25, 0.10, 96.25), -PI * 0.5, "van")
    _create_vehicle(15, Vector3(516.25, 0.10, 101.25), -PI * 0.5, "z_type")
    _create_vehicle(16, Vector3(526.25, 0.10, 101.25), -PI * 0.5, "z_type")
    _create_vehicle(17, Vector3(531.25, 0.10, 96.25), -PI * 0.5, "tank")
    # Dedicated, non-traffic test tank within interaction range of HOST. It is
    # offset to the west so it neither overlaps the player nor the weapon row.
    _create_vehicle(18, Vector3(320.00, 0.10, 323.75), PI * 0.5, "tank")
    if vehicles.has(15):
        vehicles[15].set_meta("gang_id", GANG_DATA.ZAIBATSU)
    if vehicles.has(16):
        vehicles[16].set_meta("gang_id", GANG_DATA.ZAIBATSU)

    if population_vehicle_routes.is_empty():
        return
    var variants: Array[String] = [
        "beamer","bug","miara","meteor","van","taxi_xpress","hot_dog_van","ice_cream_van",
        "taxi","hachura","maurice","wellard","rumbler","trance_am","gt_a1","benson",
        "bus","pacifier","truck_cab_sx","box_truck"
    ]
    for i in range(AMBIENT_TRAFFIC_POOL_SIZE):
        var route_index: int = (i * 17) % population_vehicle_routes.size()
        var route: Array[Vector3] = _route_from_variant(population_vehicle_routes[route_index])
        if route.size() < 2:
            continue
        var start_position: Vector3 = route[0]
        var delta: Vector3 = route[1] - route[0]
        var heading: float = atan2(delta.z, delta.x) if delta.length_squared() > 0.001 else 0.0
        var vehicle_id: int = 100 + i
        _create_traffic_vehicle(vehicle_id, start_position, heading, route, 10.2 + float(i % 5) * 0.45, variants[i % variants.size()], false, true)
        if vehicles.has(vehicle_id):
            var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
            vehicle.set_ambient_pool_member(true)
            vehicle.set_stream_active(false)
            vehicle_stream_states[vehicle_id] = false
            ambient_vehicle_ids.append(vehicle_id)


--- lines 805-820 ---
func _create_vehicle(id: int, spawn: Vector3, start_heading: float, variant: String = "jefferson", pooled: bool = false) -> void:
    var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = VEHICLE_SCRIPT.new() as ViceQuestVehicle
    vehicle.name = "Vehicle_%d" % id
    vehicle.ambient_pool_member = pooled
    vehicle.defer_runtime_resources = pooled
    vehicle.stream_active = not pooled
    add_child(vehicle)
    # GMP-derived routes/spawns already carry their correct Z layer. Re-sampling
    # here could incorrectly snap a road vehicle onto an overlapping roof.
    var safe_spawn: Vector3 = spawn
    vehicle.setup(id, safe_spawn, start_heading)
    vehicle.set_variant(variant)
    vehicle.set_meta("base_variant", variant)
    vehicles[id] = vehicle
    vehicle_stream_states[id] = not pooled


--- lines 821-825 ---
func _create_traffic_vehicle(id: int, spawn: Vector3, start_heading: float, route: Array[Vector3], cruise_speed: float, variant: String = "jefferson", loop_route: bool = true, pooled: bool = false) -> void:
    _create_vehicle(id, spawn, start_heading, variant, pooled)
    if vehicles.has(id):
        vehicles[id].configure_traffic(route, true, cruise_speed, loop_route)

func _build_police_pursuit_pool() -> void:
    for pursuit_index in range(POLICE_PURSUIT_CAR_COUNT):
        var vehicle_id: int = 300 + pursuit_index
        _create_vehicle(vehicle_id, Vector3(-120.0 - float(pursuit_index) * 3.0, 0.10, -120.0), 0.0, "cop_car", true)

            vehicles[vehicle_id].set_meta("response_role", "police")
            vehicles[vehicle_id].set_stream_active(false)

--- lines 980-992 ---
func _create_civilian(id: int, spawn: Vector3, route: Array[Vector3], color: Color, starts_in_vehicle: bool = false, pooled: bool = false) -> void:
    var civilian: ViceQuestCivilian = CIVILIAN_SCRIPT.new() as ViceQuestCivilian
    civilian.name = "Civilian_%d" % id
    civilian.ambient_pool_member = pooled
    civilian.defer_runtime_resources = pooled
    civilian.stream_active = not pooled and not starts_in_vehicle
    civilian.in_vehicle = starts_in_vehicle
    add_child(civilian)
    civilian.setup(id, spawn, route, color)
    civilian.set_in_vehicle(starts_in_vehicle)
    civilians[id] = civilian
    civilian_stream_states[id] = not pooled and not starts_in_vehicle


--- lines 1034-1042 ---
func _vehicle_spawn_is_clear(candidate: Vector3, minimum_distance: float = 5.2) -> bool:
    for raw_vehicle_id in vehicles.keys():
        var vehicle_id: int = int(raw_vehicle_id)
        var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
        var active: bool = vehicle.driver_id != 0 or (vehicle_stream_states.has(vehicle_id) and vehicle_stream_states[vehicle_id])
        if active and not vehicle.is_destroyed and _planar_distance(candidate, vehicle.position) < minimum_distance:
            return false
    return true


--- lines 1052-1085 ---
func _vehicle_spawn_candidate(focuses: Array[Vector3], required_gang: int = GANG_DATA.NEUTRAL) -> Dictionary:
    if population_vehicle_routes.is_empty() or focuses.is_empty():
        return {}
    var attempt_count: int = POPULATION_SPAWN_ATTEMPTS * 3 if required_gang > 0 else POPULATION_SPAWN_ATTEMPTS
    for _attempt in range(attempt_count):
        var route_index: int = population_rng.randi_range(0, population_vehicle_routes.size() - 1)
        var source_route: Array[Vector3] = _route_from_variant(population_vehicle_routes[route_index])
        var minimum_route_size: int = 8 if required_gang > 0 else 18
        if source_route.size() < minimum_route_size:
            continue
        var reserved_tail: int = 6 if required_gang > 0 else 16
        var max_start: int = maxi(0, source_route.size() - reserved_tail)
        var start_index: int = population_rng.randi_range(0, max_start)
        var candidate: Vector3 = source_route[start_index]
        var focus_distance: float = _nearest_focus_distance(candidate, focuses)
        var minimum_radius: float = 18.0 if required_gang > 0 else TRAFFIC_SPAWN_MIN_RADIUS
        var maximum_radius: float = 82.0 if required_gang > 0 else TRAFFIC_SPAWN_MAX_RADIUS
        if focus_distance < minimum_radius or focus_distance > maximum_radius:
            continue
        if required_gang > 0 and GANG_DATA.downtown_gang_at(candidate) != required_gang:
            continue
        if not _vehicle_spawn_is_clear(candidate):
            continue
        var route: Array[Vector3] = []
        for route_point_index in range(start_index, source_route.size()):
            route.append(source_route[route_point_index])
        if route.size() < (6 if required_gang > 0 else 12):
            continue
        var delta: Vector3 = route[1] - route[0]
        if delta.length_squared() <= 0.001:
            continue
        return {"position": candidate, "heading": atan2(delta.z, delta.x), "route": route}
    return {}


--- lines 1109-1136 ---
func _activate_ambient_vehicle(vehicle_id: int, focuses: Array[Vector3], forced_gang: int = GANG_DATA.NEUTRAL) -> bool:
    if not vehicles.has(vehicle_id):
        return false
    var spawn_data: Dictionary = _vehicle_spawn_candidate(focuses, forced_gang)
    if spawn_data.is_empty():
        return false
    var route: Array[Vector3] = _route_from_variant(spawn_data["route"])
    if route.size() < 2:
        return false
    var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
    var spawn_position: Vector3 = spawn_data["position"]
    var spawn_heading: float = float(spawn_data["heading"])
    var cruise_speed: float = 9.8 + population_rng.randf_range(0.0, 2.8)
    _configure_vehicle_gang(vehicle, spawn_position, vehicle_id, forced_gang)
    vehicle.recycle_ambient(spawn_position, spawn_heading, route, cruise_speed, vehicle.variant_id)
    vehicle_stream_states[vehicle_id] = true
    _sync_ambient_vehicle_activation.rpc(vehicle_id, vehicle.position, vehicle.heading, vehicle.current_speed, vehicle.variant_id, int(vehicle.get_meta("gang_id", 0)))
    _sync_vehicle_tuning.rpc(vehicle_id, vehicle.paint_index, vehicle.paint_color, vehicle.has_vehicle_gun, vehicle.has_mines, vehicle.has_oil_slick)
    if traffic_driver_by_vehicle.has(vehicle_id):
        var driver_id: int = traffic_driver_by_vehicle[vehicle_id]
        if civilians.has(driver_id):
            var driver: ViceQuestCivilian = civilians[driver_id]
            driver.position = spawn_position
            driver.target_position = spawn_position
            driver.set_in_vehicle(true)
            civilian_stream_states[driver_id] = false
    return true


--- lines 1161-1173 ---
func _configure_vehicle_gang(vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle, world_position: Vector3, stable_seed: int, forced_gang: int = GANG_DATA.NEUTRAL) -> void:
    var territory_gang: int = GANG_DATA.downtown_gang_at(world_position)
    var assigned_gang: int = forced_gang
    if assigned_gang <= 0:
        assigned_gang = territory_gang if territory_gang > 0 and posmod(stable_seed * 19 + roundi(world_position.x), 5) < 2 else GANG_DATA.NEUTRAL
    vehicle.set_meta("gang_id", assigned_gang)
    if assigned_gang > 0:
        var gang_variant: String = GANG_DATA.vehicle_for(assigned_gang, stable_seed)
        if not gang_variant.is_empty():
            vehicle.set_variant(gang_variant)
    else:
        vehicle.set_variant(String(vehicle.get_meta("base_variant", "jefferson")))


--- lines 1174-1179 ---
func _deactivate_ambient_vehicle(vehicle_id: int) -> void:
    if vehicles.has(vehicle_id):
        vehicles[vehicle_id].set_stream_active(false)
    vehicle_stream_states[vehicle_id] = false
    _sync_vehicle_stream_state.rpc(vehicle_id, false)


--- lines 1186-1253 ---
func _try_continue_ambient_vehicle_route(vehicle_id: int) -> bool:
    if not vehicles.has(vehicle_id) or population_vehicle_routes.is_empty():
        return false
    var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
    var current_position: Vector3 = vehicle.position
    var forward: Vector3 = Vector3(cos(vehicle.heading), 0.0, sin(vehicle.heading)).normalized()
    var cardinal_x: int = 0
    var cardinal_z: int = 0
    if absf(forward.x) >= absf(forward.z):
        cardinal_x = 1 if forward.x >= 0.0 else -1
    else:
        cardinal_z = 1 if forward.z >= 0.0 else -1
    var cache_key: String = "%d:%d:%d:%d:%d" % [
        roundi(current_position.x * 10.0), roundi(current_position.y * 10.0),
        roundi(current_position.z * 10.0), cardinal_x, cardinal_z
    ]
    if route_continuation_cache.has(cache_key):
        var cached_route: Array[Vector3] = _route_from_variant(route_continuation_cache[cache_key])
        if cached_route.is_empty():
            return false
        cached_route.push_front(current_position)
        vehicle.configure_traffic(cached_route, true, vehicle.ai_cruise_speed, false)
        return true
    var best_route: Array[Vector3] = []
    var best_score: float = INF
    # Route segments are importer chunks of GTA2's Green-Arrow network, not logical dead ends.
    for route_index in range(population_vehicle_routes.size()):
        var source_route: Array[Vector3] = _route_from_variant(population_vehicle_routes[route_index])
        if source_route.size() < 10:
            continue
        var nearest_index: int = -1
        var nearest_distance: float = 3.0
        for point_index in range(source_route.size() - 1):
            if absf(current_position.y - source_route[point_index].y) > 0.20:
                continue
            var distance: float = _planar_distance(current_position, source_route[point_index])
            if distance < nearest_distance:
                nearest_distance = distance
                nearest_index = point_index
        if nearest_index < 0 or nearest_index >= source_route.size() - 6:
            continue
        var next_direction: Vector3 = source_route[nearest_index + 1] - source_route[nearest_index]
        next_direction.y = 0.0
        if next_direction.length_squared() <= 0.001:
            continue
        next_direction = next_direction.normalized()
        var alignment: float = forward.dot(next_direction)
        if alignment < 0.15:
            continue
        var score: float = nearest_distance + (1.0 - alignment) * 3.5
        if score >= best_score:
            continue
        var candidate_route: Array[Vector3] = [current_position]
        for point_index in range(nearest_index + 1, source_route.size()):
            candidate_route.append(source_route[point_index])
        if candidate_route.size() < 8:
            continue
        best_score = score
        best_route = candidate_route
    if best_route.is_empty():
        route_continuation_cache[cache_key] = []
        return false
    var cached_tail: Array[Vector3] = best_route.duplicate()
    cached_tail.pop_front()
    route_continuation_cache[cache_key] = cached_tail
    vehicle.configure_traffic(best_route, true, vehicle.ai_cruise_speed, false)
    return true


--- lines 1254-1266 ---
func _request_ambient_vehicle_recycle(vehicle_id: int) -> void:
    if not multiplayer.is_server() or not ambient_vehicle_ids.has(vehicle_id) or not vehicles.has(vehicle_id):
        return
    var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
    if vehicle.driver_id != 0 or not vehicle.ai_controlled:
        return
    if _try_continue_ambient_vehicle_route(vehicle_id):
        return
    # Never recycle a car while it is still in the player's local presentation window.
    if _is_near_any_player(vehicle.position, TRAFFIC_ROUTE_END_MIN_RADIUS):
        return
    _deactivate_ambient_vehicle(vehicle_id)


--- lines 1267-1321 ---
func _ensure_gang_vehicle_quotas(focuses: Array[Vector3]) -> void:
    var focuses_by_gang: Dictionary = {}
    for focus: Vector3 in focuses:
        var focus_gang_id: int = GANG_DATA.downtown_gang_at(focus)
        if focus_gang_id <= 0:
            continue
        if not focuses_by_gang.has(focus_gang_id):
            focuses_by_gang[focus_gang_id] = []
        var stored_focuses: Array = focuses_by_gang[focus_gang_id]
        stored_focuses.append(focus)
        focuses_by_gang[focus_gang_id] = stored_focuses
    var quota: int = QUEST_GANG_VEHICLES_PER_TERRITORY if runtime_low_power else DESKTOP_GANG_VEHICLES_PER_TERRITORY
    for raw_gang_id: Variant in focuses_by_gang.keys():
        var quota_gang_id: int = int(raw_gang_id)
        var untyped_focuses: Array = focuses_by_gang[raw_gang_id]
        var typed_focuses: Array[Vector3] = []
        for raw_focus: Variant in untyped_focuses:
            if raw_focus is Vector3:
                typed_focuses.append(raw_focus)
        var active_count: int = 0
        for vehicle_id: int in ambient_vehicle_ids:
            if not vehicles.has(vehicle_id) or not vehicle_stream_states.get(vehicle_id, false):
                continue
            var active_vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
            if int(active_vehicle.get_meta("gang_id", 0)) == quota_gang_id:
                var territory_distance: float = _nearest_focus_distance(active_vehicle.position, typed_focuses)
                if territory_distance <= 115.0:
                    active_count += 1
                elif territory_distance > 145.0 and active_vehicle.driver_id == 0:
                    _deactivate_ambient_vehicle(vehicle_id)
        var needed: int = maxi(0, quota - active_count)
        if needed <= 0:
            continue
        var candidates: Array[int] = []
        # Sleeping pool members are ideal. Active neutral cars outside the
        # immediate camera window are recycled only when the pool is saturated.
        for vehicle_id: int in ambient_vehicle_ids:
            if not vehicles.has(vehicle_id) or vehicles[vehicle_id].driver_id != 0:
                continue
            var candidate_vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
            if candidate_vehicle.is_destroyed or String(candidate_vehicle.get_meta("response_role", "")) == "police":
                continue
            var is_active: bool = bool(vehicle_stream_states.get(vehicle_id, false))
            if not is_active:
                candidates.push_front(vehicle_id)
            elif int(candidate_vehicle.get_meta("gang_id", 0)) == GANG_DATA.NEUTRAL and _nearest_focus_distance(candidate_vehicle.position, focuses) >= 28.0:
                candidates.append(vehicle_id)
        for vehicle_id: int in candidates:
            if needed <= 0:
                break
            if vehicle_stream_states.get(vehicle_id, false):
                _deactivate_ambient_vehicle(vehicle_id)
            if _activate_ambient_vehicle(vehicle_id, typed_focuses, quota_gang_id):
                needed -= 1


--- lines 1511-1527 ---
func _sync_ambient_vehicle_activation(vehicle_id: int, spawn_position: Vector3, spawn_heading: float, vehicle_speed: float, variant_id: String = "jefferson", gang_id: int = 0) -> void:
    if not vehicles.has(vehicle_id):
        return
    var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
    vehicle.position = spawn_position
    vehicle.target_position = spawn_position
    vehicle.heading = spawn_heading
    vehicle.target_heading = spawn_heading
    vehicle.rotation.y = -spawn_heading
    vehicle.current_speed = vehicle_speed
    vehicle.set_variant(variant_id)
    vehicle.set_meta("gang_id", gang_id)
    vehicle.ai_controlled = true
    vehicle_stream_states[vehicle_id] = true
    vehicle.set_stream_active(true)

--- lines 1654-1677 ---
func _load_gta2_collision_shape(path: String) -> ConcavePolygonShape3D:
    var file: FileAccess = FileAccess.open(path, FileAccess.READ)
    if file == null:
        return null
    var vertex_count: int = int(file.get_32())
    if vertex_count <= 0:
        return null
    var payload: PackedByteArray = file.get_buffer(file.get_length() - file.get_position())
    var values: PackedFloat32Array = payload.to_float32_array()
    if values.size() < vertex_count * 3:
        return null
    var faces: PackedVector3Array = PackedVector3Array()
    faces.resize(vertex_count)
    for i in range(vertex_count):
        var base: int = i * 3
        faces[i] = Vector3(values[base], values[base + 1], values[base + 2])
    var shape: ConcavePolygonShape3D = ConcavePolygonShape3D.new()
    shape.set_faces(faces)
    # GTA2 walls are gameplay boundaries, not one-way polygons. The old trimesh
    # was effectively one-sided, which let the player enter a building from the
    # back face and then trapped them on the way out.
    shape.backface_collision = true
    return shape


--- lines 1720-1744 ---
func _desired_collision_chunks() -> Dictionary:
    var desired: Dictionary = {}
    if multiplayer.is_server() and not players.is_empty():
        for raw_player_id in players.keys():
            var player_id: int = int(raw_player_id)
            var focus: Vector3 = players[player_id].position
            if player_vehicle.has(player_id) and vehicles.has(player_vehicle[player_id]):
                focus = vehicles[player_vehicle[player_id]].position
            _add_chunk_neighborhood(desired, focus, COLLISION_CHUNK_RADIUS)
        # Active population can briefly lead or trail the player window. Keep
        # its exact physics chunk present, without loading the full map.
        for raw_vehicle_id in vehicles.keys():
            var vehicle_id: int = int(raw_vehicle_id)
            if bool(vehicle_stream_states.get(vehicle_id, false)) or vehicles[vehicle_id].driver_id != 0:
                desired[_chunk_for_position(vehicles[vehicle_id].position)] = true
        for raw_civilian_id in civilians.keys():
            var civilian_id: int = int(raw_civilian_id)
            if bool(civilian_stream_states.get(civilian_id, false)):
                desired[_chunk_for_position(civilians[civilian_id].position)] = true
    else:
        _add_chunk_neighborhood(desired, _local_stream_focus(), COLLISION_CHUNK_RADIUS)
    if desired.is_empty():
        _add_chunk_neighborhood(desired, DOWNTOWN_DATA.HOST, COLLISION_CHUNK_RADIUS)
    return desired

func _ensure_collision_chunk(coord: Vector2i) -> void:
    if gta2_collision_chunks.has(coord):
        return
    var path: String = "res://assets/gta2/downtown/downtown_collision_%d_%d.colbin" % [coord.x, coord.y]
    var collision_shape: ConcavePolygonShape3D = _load_gta2_collision_shape(path)
    if collision_shape == null:
        return
    var body: StaticBody3D = StaticBody3D.new()
    body.name = "GTA2Collision_%d_%d" % [coord.x, coord.y]
    body.collision_layer = 1
    body.collision_mask = 0
    var shape_node: CollisionShape3D = CollisionShape3D.new()
    shape_node.shape = collision_shape
    body.add_child(shape_node)
    add_child(body)
    gta2_collision_chunks[coord] = body

func _create_road(center: Vector3, size: Vector3, horizontal: bool) -> void:
    _create_textured_surface(center, size, "res://assets/city/asphalt.png", 1.0)
    # Four lanes: double yellow center line and dashed white separators.
    if horizontal:
        _create_surface(center + Vector3(0.0, 0.055, -0.10), Vector3(size.x - 0.4, 0.025, 0.055), Color("d2a936"))
        _create_surface(center + Vector3(0.0, 0.055, 0.10), Vector3(size.x - 0.4, 0.025, 0.055), Color("d2a936"))
        _create_lane_dashes(center, size.x, true, -LANE_DIVIDER_OFFSET)
        _create_lane_dashes(center, size.x, true, LANE_DIVIDER_OFFSET)
    else:
        _create_surface(center + Vector3(-0.10, 0.055, 0.0), Vector3(0.055, 0.025, size.z - 0.4), Color("d2a936"))
        _create_surface(center + Vector3(0.10, 0.055, 0.0), Vector3(0.055, 0.025, size.z - 0.4), Color("d2a936"))
        _create_lane_dashes(center, size.z, false, -LANE_DIVIDER_OFFSET)
        _create_lane_dashes(center, size.z, false, LANE_DIVIDER_OFFSET)


--- lines 2013-2023 ---
func _create_lane_dashes(center: Vector3, length: float, horizontal: bool, lane_offset: float) -> void:
    var step: float = 3.4
    var dash_length: float = 1.45
    var cursor: float = -length * 0.5 + 1.0
    while cursor < length * 0.5 - 1.0:
        if horizontal:
            _create_surface(center + Vector3(cursor, 0.058, lane_offset), Vector3(dash_length, 0.026, 0.055), Color("d9dedb"))
        else:
            _create_surface(center + Vector3(lane_offset, 0.058, cursor), Vector3(0.055, 0.026, dash_length), Color("d9dedb"))
        cursor += step

--- lines 3276-3300 ---
func _weapon_ray_hit(shooter: ViceQuestPlayer, attacker_id: int, start: Vector3, direction: Vector3, weapon_range: float, damage: int, vehicle_damage: int) -> Vector3:
    var end: Vector3 = start + direction * weapon_range
    var query: PhysicsRayQueryParameters3D = PhysicsRayQueryParameters3D.create(start, end)
    query.exclude = [shooter.get_rid()]
    query.collision_mask = 3
    query.collide_with_areas = false
    var hit: Dictionary = get_world_3d().direct_space_state.intersect_ray(query)
    if hit.is_empty():
        return end
    var hit_position: Vector3 = hit["position"]
    var collider: Object = hit["collider"]
    if collider is ViceQuestPlayer:
        var target: ViceQuestPlayer = collider as ViceQuestPlayer
        if target.peer_id != attacker_id and alive_states.has(target.peer_id) and alive_states[target.peer_id]:
            _damage_player(target.peer_id, damage, attacker_id)
    elif collider is ViceQuestVehicle:
        var hit_vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = collider as ViceQuestVehicle
        if not hit_vehicle.is_destroyed:
            _damage_vehicle(hit_vehicle.vehicle_id, vehicle_damage, attacker_id)
    elif collider != null and collider.has_meta("cop_id"):
        _damage_cop(int(collider.get_meta("cop_id")), damage, attacker_id)
    elif collider != null and collider.has_meta("civilian_id"):
        _damage_civilian(int(collider.get_meta("civilian_id")), damage, attacker_id)
    return hit_position


--- lines 3301-3340 ---
func _apply_weapon_explosion(blast_position: Vector3, radius: float, max_damage: int, attacker_id: int) -> void:
    for raw_player_id in players.keys():
        var player_id: int = int(raw_player_id)
        if alive_states.has(player_id) and alive_states[player_id]:
            var distance: float = _planar_distance(players[player_id].position, blast_position)
            if distance <= radius:
                if not player_vehicle.has(player_id):
                    players[player_id].apply_knockback(_radial_impulse(blast_position, players[player_id].position, radius, 12.0))
                _damage_player(player_id, maxi(1, int(round(float(max_damage) * (1.0 - clampf(distance / radius, 0.0, 1.0))))), attacker_id)
    for raw_vehicle_id in vehicles.keys():
        var vehicle_id: int = int(raw_vehicle_id)
        var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
        if not vehicle.is_destroyed:
            var distance: float = _planar_distance(vehicle.position, blast_position)
            if distance <= radius:
                vehicle.apply_external_impulse(_radial_impulse(blast_position, vehicle.position, radius, 6.5))
                _damage_vehicle(vehicle_id, maxi(1, int(round(float(max_damage) * (1.0 - clampf(distance / radius, 0.0, 1.0))))), attacker_id)
    for raw_cop_id in cops.keys():
        var cop_id: int = int(raw_cop_id)
        var cop: ViceQuestCop = cops[cop_id]
        if cop.is_alive:
            var distance: float = _planar_distance(cop.position, blast_position)
            if distance <= radius:
                cop.apply_knockback(_radial_impulse(blast_position, cop.position, radius, 12.0))
                _damage_cop(cop_id, maxi(1, int(round(float(max_damage) * (1.0 - clampf(distance / radius, 0.0, 1.0))))), attacker_id)
                if cop.is_alive:
                    cop.apply_unconscious(3.6, blast_position)
    for raw_civilian_id in civilians.keys():
        var civilian_id: int = int(raw_civilian_id)
        var civilian: ViceQuestCivilian = civilians[civilian_id]
        if civilian.is_alive and not civilian.in_vehicle:
            var distance: float = _planar_distance(civilian.position, blast_position)
            if distance <= radius:
                civilian.apply_knockback(_radial_impulse(blast_position, civilian.position, radius, 14.0))
                _damage_civilian(civilian_id, maxi(1, int(round(float(max_damage) * (1.0 - clampf(distance / radius, 0.0, 1.0))))), attacker_id)
                if civilian.is_alive:
                    civilian.apply_unconscious(3.8, blast_position)
    _play_vehicle_explosion.rpc(blast_position, radius)
    _panic_civilians(blast_position, radius * 3.0)


--- lines 3357-3384 ---
func _weapon_stun_hit(shooter: ViceQuestPlayer, attacker_id: int, start: Vector3, direction: Vector3, weapon_range: float, damage: int) -> Vector3:
    var end: Vector3 = start + direction * weapon_range
    var query: PhysicsRayQueryParameters3D = PhysicsRayQueryParameters3D.create(start, end)
    query.exclude = [shooter.get_rid()]
    query.collision_mask = 3
    query.collide_with_areas = false
    var hit: Dictionary = get_world_3d().direct_space_state.intersect_ray(query)
    if hit.is_empty():
        return end
    var collider: Object = hit["collider"]
    if collider is ViceQuestPlayer:
        var target: ViceQuestPlayer = collider as ViceQuestPlayer
        if target.peer_id != attacker_id:
            player_stun_timers[target.peer_id] = 2.2
            _damage_player(target.peer_id, damage, attacker_id)
    elif collider != null and collider.has_meta("cop_id"):
        var cop_id: int = int(collider.get_meta("cop_id"))
        _damage_cop(cop_id, damage, attacker_id)
        if cops.has(cop_id) and cops[cop_id].is_alive:
            cops[cop_id].apply_unconscious(3.4, start)
    elif collider != null and collider.has_meta("civilian_id"):
        var civilian_id: int = int(collider.get_meta("civilian_id"))
        if civilians.has(civilian_id):
            _damage_civilian(civilian_id, damage, attacker_id)
            if civilians[civilian_id].is_alive:
                civilians[civilian_id].apply_unconscious(3.6, start)
    return hit["position"]

--- lines 3629-3651 ---
func _apply_fire_zone_damage(state: Dictionary) -> void:
    var center: Vector3 = state["position"]
    var radius: float = float(state["radius"])
    var damage: int = maxi(1, roundi(float(state["damage"]) * 0.28))
    var attacker_id: int = int(state["attacker_id"])
    for raw_player_id in players.keys():
        var player_id: int = int(raw_player_id)
        if alive_states.get(player_id, false) and _planar_distance(players[player_id].position, center) <= radius:
            _ignite_entity("player", player_id, attacker_id, 3.0, maxi(4, damage))
    for raw_vehicle_id in vehicles.keys():
        var vehicle_id: int = int(raw_vehicle_id)
        if not vehicles[vehicle_id].is_destroyed and _planar_distance(vehicles[vehicle_id].position, center) <= radius:
            _damage_vehicle(vehicle_id, maxi(1, roundi(float(damage) * 0.5)), attacker_id)
    for raw_cop_id in cops.keys():
        var cop_id: int = int(raw_cop_id)
        if cops[cop_id].is_alive and _planar_distance(cops[cop_id].position, center) <= radius:
            _ignite_entity("cop", cop_id, attacker_id, 3.0, maxi(4, damage))
    for raw_civilian_id in civilians.keys():
        var civilian_id: int = int(raw_civilian_id)
        var civilian: ViceQuestCivilian = civilians[civilian_id]
        if civilian.is_alive and not civilian.in_vehicle and _planar_distance(civilian.position, center) <= radius:
            _ignite_entity("civilian", civilian_id, attacker_id, 3.2, maxi(5, damage))


--- lines 3672-3702 ---
func _ignite_entity(kind: String, entity_id: int, attacker_id: int, duration: float, tick_damage: int) -> void:
    if not _burn_target_alive(kind, entity_id):
        return
    var burn_key: String = "%s:%d" % [kind, entity_id]
    var is_new: bool = not active_burns.has(burn_key)
    var burn_cap: int = 16 if runtime_low_power else 28
    if is_new and active_burns.size() >= burn_cap:
        var oldest_key: String = String(active_burns.keys()[0])
        active_burns.erase(oldest_key)
        _set_burning_visual.rpc(oldest_key, false)
    var state: Dictionary = active_burns.get(burn_key, {})
    state["kind"] = kind
    state["id"] = entity_id
    state["attacker_id"] = attacker_id
    state["timer"] = maxf(float(state.get("timer", 0.0)), duration)
    state["tick"] = minf(float(state.get("tick", 0.25)), 0.25)
    state["damage"] = maxi(int(state.get("damage", 0)), tick_damage)
    active_burns[burn_key] = state
    if kind == "civilian" and civilians.has(entity_id):
        var threat_position: Vector3 = civilians[entity_id].position - civilians[entity_id].facing
        if players.has(attacker_id):
            threat_position = players[attacker_id].position
        civilians[entity_id].set_burning_state(true, threat_position, duration)
    elif kind == "cop" and cops.has(entity_id):
        var cop_threat: Vector3 = cops[entity_id].position - cops[entity_id].facing
        if players.has(attacker_id):
            cop_threat = players[attacker_id].position
        cops[entity_id].set_burning_state(true, cop_threat, duration)
    if is_new:
        _set_burning_visual.rpc(burn_key, true)


--- lines 3811-3866 ---
func _server_fire_vehicle_weapon(player_id: int) -> void:
    if not player_vehicle.has(player_id):
        return
    var vehicle_id: int = player_vehicle[player_id]
    if not vehicles.has(vehicle_id):
        return
    var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
    if vehicle.variant_id == "tank":
        if vehicle.is_destroyed:
            return
        var tank_cooldown: float = float(shot_cooldowns.get(player_id, 0.0))
        if tank_cooldown > 0.0:
            return
        shot_cooldowns[player_id] = 1.05
        var tank_direction: Vector3 = vehicle.get_turret_forward_vector()
        var tank_start: Vector3 = vehicle.position + Vector3(0.0, 0.48, 0.0) + tank_direction * 1.55
        var cannon_def: Dictionary = WEAPON_DATA.definition(WEAPON_DATA.ROCKET_LAUNCHER).duplicate(true)
        cannon_def["projectile_speed"] = 22.0
        cannon_def["fuse"] = 2.4
        cannon_def["explosion_radius"] = 5.6
        cannon_def["explosion_damage"] = 115
        _spawn_combat_projectile(player_id, WEAPON_DATA.ROCKET_LAUNCHER, tank_start, tank_direction, cannon_def)
        _play_shot_effect.rpc(player_id, tank_start, tank_start + tank_direction * 1.6)
        _panic_civilians(tank_start, 24.0)
        return
    if not vehicle.has_vehicle_gun or vehicle.is_destroyed:
        _show_combat_message.rpc_id(player_id, "VEHICLE // No mounted gun installed")
        return
    var cooldown: float = float(shot_cooldowns.get(player_id, 0.0))
    if cooldown > 0.0:
        return
    shot_cooldowns[player_id] = VEHICLE_GUN_COOLDOWN
    var direction: Vector3 = vehicle.get_forward_vector()
    var start: Vector3 = vehicle.position + Vector3(0.0, 0.42, 0.0) + direction * 1.05
    var end: Vector3 = start + direction * VEHICLE_GUN_RANGE
    var query: PhysicsRayQueryParameters3D = PhysicsRayQueryParameters3D.create(start, end)
    query.exclude = [vehicle.get_rid()]
    query.collision_mask = 3
    query.collide_with_areas = false
    var hit: Dictionary = get_world_3d().direct_space_state.intersect_ray(query)
    if not hit.is_empty():
        end = hit["position"]
        var collider: Object = hit["collider"]
        if collider is ViceQuestPlayer:
            var target: ViceQuestPlayer = collider as ViceQuestPlayer
            if target.peer_id != player_id:
                _damage_player(target.peer_id, VEHICLE_GUN_DAMAGE, player_id)
        elif collider is ViceQuestVehicle:
            _damage_vehicle((collider as ViceQuestVehicle).vehicle_id, VEHICLE_GUN_DAMAGE, player_id)
        elif collider != null and collider.has_meta("cop_id"):
            _damage_cop(int(collider.get_meta("cop_id")), VEHICLE_GUN_DAMAGE, player_id)
        elif collider != null and collider.has_meta("civilian_id"):
            _damage_civilian(int(collider.get_meta("civilian_id")), VEHICLE_GUN_DAMAGE, player_id)
    _play_shot_effect.rpc(player_id, start, end)
    _panic_civilians(start, 15.0)

--- lines 3880-3913 ---
func _server_deploy_vehicle_gadget(player_id: int, gadget: String) -> void:
    if not player_vehicle.has(player_id):
        return
    var vehicle_id: int = player_vehicle[player_id]
    if not vehicles.has(vehicle_id):
        return
    var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
    var drop_position: Vector3 = vehicle.position - vehicle.get_forward_vector() * 1.25
    drop_position.y = sample_surface_height(drop_position, vehicle.position.y - 0.10) + 0.045
    var gadget_id: int = next_vehicle_gadget_id
    next_vehicle_gadget_id += 1
    if gadget == "mine":
        if not vehicle.has_mines:
            _show_combat_message.rpc_id(player_id, "VEHICLE // Mine dispenser not installed")
            return
        _trim_vehicle_gadget_pool(active_vehicle_mines, true, MAX_ACTIVE_MINES)
        active_vehicle_mines[gadget_id] = {
            "position": drop_position, "owner": player_id, "owner_vehicle": vehicle_id,
            "arm": 0.65, "grace": 1.8, "life": VEHICLE_MINE_LIFETIME,
        }
        _spawn_vehicle_gadget_visual.rpc(gadget_id, "mine", drop_position)
        _show_combat_message.rpc_id(player_id, "MINE DEPLOYED")
    elif gadget == "oil":
        if not vehicle.has_oil_slick:
            _show_combat_message.rpc_id(player_id, "VEHICLE // Oil slick not installed")
            return
        _trim_vehicle_gadget_pool(active_oil_slicks, false, MAX_ACTIVE_OIL_SLICKS)
        active_oil_slicks[gadget_id] = {
            "position": drop_position, "owner": player_id, "life": VEHICLE_OIL_LIFETIME,
            "affected": {},
        }
        _spawn_vehicle_gadget_visual.rpc(gadget_id, "oil", drop_position)
        _show_combat_message.rpc_id(player_id, "OIL SLICK DEPLOYED")


--- lines 3914-3919 ---
func _trim_vehicle_gadget_pool(pool: Dictionary, mine_pool: bool, maximum: int) -> void:
    while pool.size() >= maximum:
        var oldest_id: int = int(pool.keys()[0])
        pool.erase(oldest_id)
        _remove_vehicle_gadget_visual.rpc(oldest_id, "mine" if mine_pool else "oil")


--- lines 3920-3979 ---
func _server_vehicle_gadget_step(delta: float) -> void:
    var finished_mines: Array[int] = []
    for raw_id: Variant in active_vehicle_mines.keys():
        var gadget_id: int = int(raw_id)
        var state: Dictionary = active_vehicle_mines[gadget_id]
        state["arm"] = maxf(0.0, float(state["arm"]) - delta)
        state["grace"] = maxf(0.0, float(state["grace"]) - delta)
        state["life"] = float(state["life"]) - delta
        active_vehicle_mines[gadget_id] = state
        if float(state["life"]) <= 0.0:
            finished_mines.append(gadget_id)
            continue
        if float(state["arm"]) > 0.0:
            continue
        var mine_position: Vector3 = state["position"]
        for raw_vehicle_id: Variant in vehicles.keys():
            var vehicle_id: int = int(raw_vehicle_id)
            var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
            if vehicle.is_destroyed or not vehicle.stream_active:
                continue
            if vehicle_id == int(state["owner_vehicle"]) and float(state["grace"]) > 0.0:
                continue
            if _planar_distance(vehicle.position, mine_position) <= 1.25:
                _apply_weapon_explosion(mine_position, 3.4, 72, int(state["owner"]))
                finished_mines.append(gadget_id)
                break
    for gadget_id: int in finished_mines:
        active_vehicle_mines.erase(gadget_id)
        _remove_vehicle_gadget_visual.rpc(gadget_id, "mine")

    var finished_oil: Array[int] = []
    for raw_id: Variant in active_oil_slicks.keys():
        var gadget_id: int = int(raw_id)
        var state: Dictionary = active_oil_slicks[gadget_id]
        state["life"] = float(state["life"]) - delta
        var affected: Dictionary = state["affected"]
        for raw_affected_id: Variant in affected.keys():
            var affected_id: int = int(raw_affected_id)
            affected[affected_id] = float(affected[affected_id]) - delta
            if float(affected[affected_id]) <= 0.0:
                affected.erase(affected_id)
        state["affected"] = affected
        active_oil_slicks[gadget_id] = state
        if float(state["life"]) <= 0.0:
            finished_oil.append(gadget_id)
            continue
        var oil_position: Vector3 = state["position"]
        for raw_vehicle_id: Variant in vehicles.keys():
            var vehicle_id: int = int(raw_vehicle_id)
            var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
            if vehicle.is_destroyed or not vehicle.stream_active or affected.has(vehicle_id):
                continue
            if _planar_distance(vehicle.position, oil_position) <= 1.55:
                vehicle.apply_oil_slip(gadget_id + vehicle_id)
                affected[vehicle_id] = 1.1
    for gadget_id: int in finished_oil:
        active_oil_slicks.erase(gadget_id)
        _remove_vehicle_gadget_visual.rpc(gadget_id, "oil")

@rpc("authority", "call_local", "reliable")

--- lines 3980-4003 ---
func _spawn_vehicle_gadget_visual(gadget_id: int, gadget: String, gadget_position: Vector3) -> void:
    var node: Node3D = Node3D.new()
    node.name = "VehicleGadget_%s_%d" % [gadget, gadget_id]
    node.position = gadget_position
    var mesh_instance: MeshInstance3D = MeshInstance3D.new()
    var mesh: CylinderMesh = CylinderMesh.new()
    mesh.height = 0.035
    mesh.radial_segments = 20
    mesh.top_radius = 0.34 if gadget == "mine" else 1.20
    mesh.bottom_radius = mesh.top_radius
    var material: StandardMaterial3D = StandardMaterial3D.new()
    material.shading_mode = BaseMaterial3D.SHADING_MODE_UNSHADED
    material.transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TRANSPARENCY_ALPHA
    material.albedo_color = Color(0.95, 0.24, 0.08, 0.95) if gadget == "mine" else Color(0.025, 0.03, 0.04, 0.82)
    mesh.material = material
    mesh_instance.mesh = mesh
    node.add_child(mesh_instance)
    add_child(node)
    if gadget == "mine":
        vehicle_mine_visuals[gadget_id] = node
    else:
        oil_slick_visuals[gadget_id] = node

--- lines 4147-4169 ---
func _damage_cop(cop_id: int, amount: int, attacker_id: int = 0, blood: bool = true) -> void:
    if not cops.has(cop_id):
        return
    var cop: ViceQuestCop = cops[cop_id]
    if not cop.is_alive:
        return
    if attacker_id > 0:
        var attacker_wanted: int = wanted_levels[attacker_id] if wanted_levels.has(attacker_id) else 0
        if attacker_wanted == 0:
            _add_wanted(attacker_id, 1, "ASSAULT ON POLICE")
    var new_hp: int = maxi(0, cop.hp - amount)
    if blood:
        var hit_direction: Vector3 = cop.position - players[attacker_id].position if players.has(attacker_id) else cop.facing
        _play_blood_fx.rpc(cop.position, hit_direction, new_hp <= 0)
    cop.set_state(new_hp, new_hp > 0)
    _sync_cop_state.rpc(cop_id, cop.position, cop.facing, cop.is_alive, cop.hp)
    if new_hp <= 0:
        if attacker_id > 0:
            _add_wanted(attacker_id, 1, "COP KILLED")
            _add_money(attacker_id, REWARD_COP, "COP DOWN")
        cop_respawn_timers[cop_id] = 10.0
        _show_combat_message.rpc("Cop down!")


--- lines 4170-4194 ---
func _damage_civilian(civilian_id: int, amount: int, attacker_id: int = 0, blood: bool = true, count_reputation: bool = true, cause_panic: bool = true) -> void:
    if not civilians.has(civilian_id):
        return
    var civilian: ViceQuestCivilian = civilians[civilian_id]
    if not civilian.is_alive:
        return
    var new_hp: int = maxi(0, civilian.hp - amount)
    if blood:
        var hit_direction: Vector3 = civilian.position - players[attacker_id].position if players.has(attacker_id) else civilian.facing
        _play_blood_fx.rpc(civilian.position, hit_direction, new_hp <= 0)
    if count_reputation and attacker_id > 0 and civilian.gang_id > 0:
        _adjust_gang_respect(attacker_id, civilian.gang_id, -2 if new_hp <= 0 else -1)
    civilian.set_state(new_hp, new_hp > 0)
    if cause_panic:
        _panic_civilians(civilian.position, 12.0)
    _sync_civilian_state.rpc(civilian_id, civilian.position, civilian.facing, civilian.is_alive, civilian.hp, civilian.in_vehicle, civilian.panic_timer > 0.0)
    if new_hp <= 0:
        civilian_respawn_timers[civilian_id] = CIVILIAN_RESPAWN_DELAY
        if attacker_id > 0 and civilian.gang_id > 0:
            _server_mission_gang_kill(attacker_id, civilian.gang_id)
            _add_money(attacker_id, REWARD_GANG_MEMBER, "GANG MEMBER DOWN")
        if attacker_id > 0:
            _add_wanted(attacker_id, 1, "CIVILIAN KILLED")
        _show_combat_message.rpc("Civilian down!")


--- lines 4305-4331 ---
func _response_spawn_point(target: Vector3, for_vehicle: bool, seed: int) -> Vector3:
    var source: Array = population_vehicle_routes if for_vehicle else population_ped_routes
    var minimum: float = 28.0 if for_vehicle else 18.0
    var maximum: float = 54.0 if for_vehicle else 34.0
    if not source.is_empty():
        for attempt in range(72):
            var route_index: int = posmod(seed * 17 + attempt * 29, source.size())
            var route: Array[Vector3] = _route_from_variant(source[route_index])
            if route.is_empty():
                continue
            var point_index: int = posmod(seed * 11 + attempt * 13, route.size())
            var candidate: Vector3 = route[point_index]
            var distance: float = _planar_distance(candidate, target)
            if distance < minimum or distance > maximum:
                continue
            if for_vehicle and not _vehicle_spawn_is_clear(candidate, 4.2):
                continue
            if not for_vehicle and not _ped_spawn_is_clear(candidate, 1.2):
                continue
            return candidate
    var angle: float = float(seed) * 2.39996
    var fallback: Vector3 = target + Vector3(cos(angle), 0.0, sin(angle)) * lerpf(minimum, maximum, 0.55)
    fallback.x = clampf(fallback.x, 1.0, WORLD_SIZE.x - 1.0)
    fallback.z = clampf(fallback.z, 1.0, WORLD_SIZE.y - 1.0)
    fallback.y = sample_surface_height(fallback, target.y) + PLAYER_Y
    return fallback


--- lines 4332-4431 ---
func _server_update_pursuit_forces(delta: float) -> void:
    police_response_accumulator += delta
    if police_response_accumulator < POLICE_RESPONSE_INTERVAL:
        return
    police_response_accumulator = 0.0
    var wanted_target_id: int = 0
    var highest_wanted: int = 0
    for raw_player_id in players.keys():
        var player_id: int = int(raw_player_id)
        var level: int = int(wanted_levels.get(player_id, 0))
        if alive_states.get(player_id, false) and level > highest_wanted:
            highest_wanted = level
            wanted_target_id = player_id
    if wanted_target_id == 0:
        for police_vehicle_id: int in police_pursuit_vehicle_ids:
            if vehicles.has(police_vehicle_id):
                vehicles[police_vehicle_id].clear_pursuit()
                vehicles[police_vehicle_id].set_stream_active(false)
                vehicle_stream_states[police_vehicle_id] = false
                _sync_vehicle_stream_state.rpc(police_vehicle_id, false)
    else:
        var target: Vector3 = players[wanted_target_id].position
        if player_vehicle.has(wanted_target_id) and vehicles.has(player_vehicle[wanted_target_id]):
            target = vehicles[player_vehicle[wanted_target_id]].position
        var desired_cops: int = mini(POLICE_RESERVE_COUNT, 2 + highest_wanted * 2)
        var active_response_cops: int = 0
        for reserve_id: int in police_response_cop_ids:
            if cops.has(reserve_id) and cops[reserve_id].is_alive and _planar_distance(cops[reserve_id].position, target) <= 58.0:
                active_response_cops += 1
        for reserve_id: int in police_response_cop_ids:
            if active_response_cops >= desired_cops:
                break
            if not cops.has(reserve_id) or not cops[reserve_id].is_alive:
                continue
            var reserve_cop: ViceQuestCop = cops[reserve_id]
            if _planar_distance(reserve_cop.position, target) <= 58.0:
                continue
            var cop_spawn: Vector3 = _response_spawn_point(target, false, reserve_id + highest_wanted * 31)
            reserve_cop.position = cop_spawn
            reserve_cop.target_position = cop_spawn
            reserve_cop.facing = (target - cop_spawn).normalized()
            _sync_cop_state.rpc(reserve_id, reserve_cop.position, reserve_cop.facing, true, reserve_cop.hp)
            active_response_cops += 1
        var desired_cars: int = mini(POLICE_PURSUIT_CAR_COUNT, 1 + highest_wanted / 2)
        for pool_index in range(police_pursuit_vehicle_ids.size()):
            var police_vehicle_id: int = police_pursuit_vehicle_ids[pool_index]
            if not vehicles.has(police_vehicle_id):
                continue
            var police_vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[police_vehicle_id]
            if pool_index >= desired_cars:
                police_vehicle.clear_pursuit()
                police_vehicle.set_stream_active(false)
                vehicle_stream_states[police_vehicle_id] = false
                _sync_vehicle_stream_state.rpc(police_vehicle_id, false)
                continue
            if not police_vehicle.stream_active or _planar_distance(police_vehicle.position, target) > 150.0:
                var vehicle_spawn: Vector3 = _response_spawn_point(target, true, police_vehicle_id + highest_wanted * 43)
                var direction: Vector3 = target - vehicle_spawn
                direction.y = 0.0
                var heading: float = atan2(direction.z, direction.x)
                var response_route: Array[Vector3] = [vehicle_spawn, target]
                police_vehicle.recycle_ambient(vehicle_spawn, heading, response_route, police_vehicle.max_forward_speed * 0.92, "cop_car")
                police_vehicle.set_meta("response_role", "police")
                vehicle_stream_states[police_vehicle_id] = true
                _sync_ambient_vehicle_activation.rpc(police_vehicle_id, police_vehicle.position, police_vehicle.heading, police_vehicle.current_speed, "cop_car", 0)
            police_vehicle.set_pursuit_target(target, "police")
    # Gang cars reuse the same lightweight pursuit steering. Only hostile
    # respect and a local player keep the pursuit active.
    for raw_vehicle_id in vehicles.keys():
        var vehicle_id: int = int(raw_vehicle_id)
        var gang_vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
        if not gang_vehicle.stream_active or gang_vehicle.driver_id != 0 or gang_vehicle.is_destroyed or String(gang_vehicle.get_meta("response_role", "")) == "police":
            continue
        var gang_id: int = int(gang_vehicle.get_meta("gang_id", 0))
        if gang_id <= 0:
            if gang_vehicle.pursuit_role == "gang":
                gang_vehicle.clear_pursuit()
            continue
        var hostile_target: int = 0
        var hostile_distance: float = 70.0
        for raw_player_id in players.keys():
            var player_id: int = int(raw_player_id)
            if not alive_states.get(player_id, false):
                continue
            if not _is_player_hostile_to_gang(player_id, gang_id):
                continue
            var player_target: Vector3 = _player_world_position(player_id)
            var distance: float = _planar_distance(gang_vehicle.position, player_target)
            if distance < hostile_distance:
                hostile_distance = distance
                hostile_target = player_id
        if hostile_target == 0:
            gang_vehicle.clear_pursuit()
        else:
            var chase_target: Vector3 = players[hostile_target].position
            if player_vehicle.has(hostile_target) and vehicles.has(player_vehicle[hostile_target]):
                chase_target = vehicles[player_vehicle[hostile_target]].position
            gang_vehicle.set_pursuit_target(chase_target, "gang")

--- lines 4497-4557 ---
func _destroy_vehicle(vehicle_id: int, attacker_id: int = 0) -> void:
    if not vehicles.has(vehicle_id):
        return
    var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
    if vehicle.is_destroyed:
        return
    var blast_position: Vector3 = vehicle.position
    var driver_id: int = vehicle.driver_id
    if attacker_id > 0:
        var vehicle_reward: int = REWARD_VEHICLE
        if String(vehicle.get_meta("response_role", "")) == "police":
            vehicle_reward = REWARD_POLICE_VEHICLE
        elif int(vehicle.get_meta("gang_id", GANG_DATA.NEUTRAL)) > GANG_DATA.NEUTRAL:
            vehicle_reward = REWARD_GANG_VEHICLE
        _add_money(attacker_id, vehicle_reward, "VEHICLE DESTROYED")

    if driver_id != 0 and alive_states.has(driver_id) and alive_states[driver_id]:
        _damage_player(driver_id, VEHICLE_EXPLOSION_DAMAGE, attacker_id if attacker_id != 0 else driver_id)
        if player_vehicle.has(driver_id):
            _server_exit_vehicle(driver_id)

    for raw_player_id in players.keys():
        var player_id: int = int(raw_player_id)
        if player_id == driver_id:
            continue
        if not alive_states.has(player_id) or not alive_states[player_id]:
            continue
        var distance: float = players[player_id].position.distance_to(blast_position)
        if distance < VEHICLE_EXPLOSION_RADIUS:
            var strength: float = 1.0 - clampf(distance / VEHICLE_EXPLOSION_RADIUS, 0.0, 1.0)
            var blast_damage: int = maxi(1, int(round(float(VEHICLE_EXPLOSION_DAMAGE) * strength)))
            players[player_id].apply_knockback(_radial_impulse(blast_position, players[player_id].position, VEHICLE_EXPLOSION_RADIUS, 11.0))
            _damage_player(player_id, blast_damage, attacker_id if attacker_id != 0 else player_id)

    for raw_cop_id in cops.keys():
        var cop_id: int = int(raw_cop_id)
        var cop: ViceQuestCop = cops[cop_id]
        var cop_distance: float = _planar_distance(cop.position, blast_position)
        if cop.is_alive and cop_distance < VEHICLE_EXPLOSION_RADIUS:
            cop.apply_knockback(_radial_impulse(blast_position, cop.position, VEHICLE_EXPLOSION_RADIUS, 11.0))
            _damage_cop(cop_id, maxi(1, roundi(float(VEHICLE_EXPLOSION_DAMAGE) * (1.0 - cop_distance / VEHICLE_EXPLOSION_RADIUS))), attacker_id)
            if cop.is_alive:
                cop.apply_unconscious(3.6, blast_position)
    for raw_civilian_id in civilians.keys():
        var civilian_id: int = int(raw_civilian_id)
        var civilian: ViceQuestCivilian = civilians[civilian_id]
        var civilian_distance: float = _planar_distance(civilian.position, blast_position)
        if civilian.is_alive and not civilian.in_vehicle and civilian_distance < VEHICLE_EXPLOSION_RADIUS:
            civilian.apply_knockback(_radial_impulse(blast_position, civilian.position, VEHICLE_EXPLOSION_RADIUS, 13.0))
            _damage_civilian(civilian_id, maxi(1, roundi(float(VEHICLE_EXPLOSION_DAMAGE) * (1.0 - civilian_distance / VEHICLE_EXPLOSION_RADIUS))), attacker_id)
            if civilian.is_alive:
                civilian.apply_unconscious(3.8, blast_position)

    vehicle.set_driver(0)
    _sync_vehicle_driver.rpc(vehicle_id, 0)
    _sync_vehicle_damage_state.rpc(vehicle_id, 0, true)
    vehicle_respawn_timers[vehicle_id] = VEHICLE_RESPAWN_DELAY
    _play_vehicle_explosion.rpc(blast_position, VEHICLE_EXPLOSION_RADIUS)
    _panic_civilians(blast_position, 16.0)
    _show_combat_message.rpc("%s #%d exploded!" % [vehicle.display_name, vehicle_id])


--- lines 4558-4574 ---
func _server_tank_crush_vehicle(tank_vehicle_id: int, target_vehicle_id: int, attacker_id: int = 0) -> void:
    if not multiplayer.is_server() or tank_vehicle_id == target_vehicle_id:
        return
    if not vehicles.has(tank_vehicle_id) or not vehicles.has(target_vehicle_id):
        return
    var tank: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[tank_vehicle_id]
    var target: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[target_vehicle_id]
    if tank.variant_id != "tank" or target.variant_id == "tank" or target.is_destroyed:
        return
    # GTA2 gives TANK special car-impact handling. Vice Quest deliberately
    # combines that rule with GTA2's hard heavy-vehicle destruction path so
    # the requested tank can physically overrun normal cars in one contact.
    target.mark_tank_crushed(true)
    _destroy_vehicle(target_vehicle_id, attacker_id)
    _sync_tank_crush.rpc(target_vehicle_id, true)

func _sync_tank_crush(vehicle_id: int, crushed: bool) -> void:
    if vehicles.has(vehicle_id):
        vehicles[vehicle_id].mark_tank_crushed(crushed)


--- lines 4579-4600 ---
func _respawn_vehicle_server(vehicle_id: int) -> void:
    if not vehicles.has(vehicle_id):
        return
    var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
    vehicle.reset_to_spawn()
    _sync_tank_crush.rpc(vehicle_id, false)
    if traffic_driver_by_vehicle.has(vehicle_id):
        var traffic_driver_id: int = traffic_driver_by_vehicle[vehicle_id]
        if civilians.has(traffic_driver_id):
            var traffic_driver: ViceQuestCivilian = civilians[traffic_driver_id]
            traffic_driver.reset_to_spawn()
            traffic_driver.position = vehicle.position
            traffic_driver.target_position = vehicle.position
            traffic_driver.set_in_vehicle(true)
            _sync_civilian_state.rpc(traffic_driver_id, traffic_driver.position, traffic_driver.facing, traffic_driver.is_alive, traffic_driver.hp, true, false)
    vehicle_respawn_timers.erase(vehicle_id)
    _sync_vehicle_driver.rpc(vehicle_id, 0)
    _sync_vehicle_damage_state.rpc(vehicle_id, vehicle.max_hp, false)
    _sync_vehicle_tuning.rpc(vehicle_id, vehicle.paint_index, vehicle.paint_color, vehicle.has_vehicle_gun, vehicle.has_mines, vehicle.has_oil_slick)
    _sync_vehicle_state.rpc(vehicle_id, vehicle.position, vehicle.heading, 0.0, vehicle.ai_controlled, vehicle.turret_heading)

func _play_vehicle_explosion(blast_position: Vector3, fx_radius: float = VEHICLE_EXPLOSION_RADIUS) -> void:
    var explosion: AnimatedSprite3D = AnimatedSprite3D.new()
    explosion.sprite_frames = _get_explosion_frames()
    explosion.animation = "explode"
    explosion.billboard = BaseMaterial3D.BILLBOARD_ENABLED
    explosion.texture_filter = BaseMaterial3D.TEXTURE_FILTER_NEAREST
    explosion.pixel_size = 0.021
    explosion.position = blast_position + Vector3(0.0, 0.62, 0.0)
    explosion.no_depth_test = true
    add_child(explosion)
    explosion.animation_finished.connect(explosion.queue_free)
    explosion.play("explode")
    if combat_fx != null:
        combat_fx.play_explosion_layer(blast_position, fx_radius)
    if audio_manager != null:
        audio_manager.play_explosion(blast_position)

    if not runtime_low_power:
        var flash: OmniLight3D = OmniLight3D.new()
        flash.position = blast_position + Vector3(0.0, 0.8, 0.0)
        flash.light_color = Color("ff9b42")
        flash.light_energy = 4.0
        flash.omni_range = 4.2
        add_child(flash)
        var flash_timer: SceneTreeTimer = get_tree().create_timer(0.16)
        flash_timer.timeout.connect(flash.queue_free)


--- lines 4648-4660 ---
func _damage_player(target_id: int, amount: int, attacker_id: int, blood: bool = true) -> void:
    if not healths.has(target_id) or not alive_states.has(target_id) or not alive_states[target_id]:
        return
    var new_hp: int = maxi(0, healths[target_id] - amount)
    if blood and players.has(target_id):
        var hit_direction: Vector3 = players[target_id].position - players[attacker_id].position if players.has(attacker_id) else players[target_id].facing
        _play_blood_fx.rpc(players[target_id].position, hit_direction, new_hp <= 0)
    healths[target_id] = new_hp
    if new_hp <= 0:
        _kill_player(target_id, attacker_id)
    else:
        _sync_health_state.rpc(target_id, new_hp, true)


--- lines 4719-4762 ---
func _play_shot_effect(shooter_id: int, start: Vector3, end: Vector3) -> void:
    if players.has(shooter_id):
        players[shooter_id].show_fire_pose()
    if audio_manager != null:
        var shot_weapon: int = WEAPON_DATA.PISTOL
        var tank_cannon: bool = false
        if shooter_id > 0:
            shot_weapon = int(player_current_weapon.get(shooter_id, WEAPON_DATA.PISTOL))
            if player_vehicle.has(shooter_id) and vehicles.has(player_vehicle[shooter_id]):
                tank_cannon = vehicles[player_vehicle[shooter_id]].variant_id == "tank"
        audio_manager.play_weapon(shot_weapon, start, tank_cannon)

    var delta: Vector3 = end - start
    var distance: float = delta.length()
    if distance <= 0.02:
        return
    var direction: Vector3 = delta.normalized()

    # Procedural additive muzzle flash. The shader draws the star/glow itself,
    # while the node only exists for a few frames.
    var muzzle: MeshInstance3D = MeshInstance3D.new()
    muzzle.mesh = _get_muzzle_flash_mesh()
    muzzle.position = start + direction * 0.08 + Vector3(0.0, 0.08, 0.0)
    muzzle.rotation_degrees.x = -90.0
    muzzle.rotation.y = atan2(direction.x, direction.z)
    add_child(muzzle)

    var flash_tween: Tween = create_tween()
    flash_tween.set_parallel(true)
    flash_tween.tween_property(muzzle, "scale", Vector3(1.45, 1.45, 1.45), 0.065)
    flash_tween.finished.connect(muzzle.queue_free)
    if not runtime_low_power:
        var flash_light: OmniLight3D = OmniLight3D.new()
        flash_light.position = muzzle.position + Vector3(0.0, 0.18, 0.0)
        flash_light.light_color = Color("ffb84c")
        flash_light.light_energy = 2.8
        flash_light.omni_range = 2.0
        add_child(flash_light)
        flash_tween.tween_property(flash_light, "light_energy", 0.0, 0.065)
        flash_tween.finished.connect(flash_light.queue_free)

    _spawn_projectile_streak(start, end)

@rpc("authority", "call_local", "reliable")

--- lines 4872-4883 ---
func _play_spread_shot_effect(shooter_id: int, start: Vector3, ends: PackedVector3Array) -> void:
    if ends.is_empty():
        return
    var center_index: int = floori(float(ends.size()) * 0.5)
    _play_shot_effect(shooter_id, start, ends[center_index])
    # Three tracers communicate the fan clearly while all seven authoritative
    # pellet rays remain server-side. This avoids seven muzzle flashes/lights.
    if center_index > 0:
        _spawn_projectile_streak(start, ends[0])
    if center_index < ends.size() - 1:
        _spawn_projectile_streak(start, ends[ends.size() - 1])

--- lines 5479-5494 ---
func _server_open_tuning_shop(player_id: int, vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle, shop: Dictionary) -> bool:
    if shop.is_empty() or vehicle == null or vehicle.driver_id != player_id or vehicle.is_destroyed:
        return false
    var shop_id: String = String(shop["id"])
    var already_waiting: bool = (
        String(player_tuning_shop_inside.get(player_id, "")) == shop_id
        and bool(player_tuning_paused.get(player_id, false))
    )
    vehicle.current_speed = 0.0
    inputs[player_id] = Vector2.ZERO
    player_tuning_shop_inside[player_id] = shop_id
    player_tuning_paused[player_id] = true
    if not already_waiting:
        _send_tuning_menu(player_id, vehicle, String(shop["name"]), true)
    return true

func _on_tuning_shop_body_entered(body: Node3D, shop_id: String) -> void:

        return
    var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = body as ViceQuestVehicle
    if vehicle.driver_id == 0:
        return
    _server_open_tuning_shop(vehicle.driver_id, vehicle, TUNING_DATA.shop_by_id(shop_id))


--- lines 5636-5655 ---
func _server_try_enter_vehicle(player_id: int) -> bool:
    if not players.has(player_id):
        return false
    var player_position: Vector3 = players[player_id].position
    var nearest_vehicle_id: int = 0
    var nearest_distance: float = VEHICLE_ENTER_RANGE
    for raw_vehicle_id in vehicles.keys():
        var vehicle_id: int = int(raw_vehicle_id)
        var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
        if vehicle.driver_id != 0 or vehicle.is_destroyed or not vehicle.stream_active or not bool(vehicle_stream_states.get(vehicle_id, false)):
            continue
        var distance: float = _planar_distance(player_position, vehicle.position)
        if distance <= nearest_distance:
            nearest_distance = distance
            nearest_vehicle_id = vehicle_id
    if nearest_vehicle_id == 0:
        return false
    _server_enter_vehicle(player_id, nearest_vehicle_id)
    return true


--- lines 5656-5675 ---
func _eject_traffic_driver(vehicle_id: int) -> void:
    if not traffic_driver_by_vehicle.has(vehicle_id) or not vehicles.has(vehicle_id):
        return
    var civilian_id: int = traffic_driver_by_vehicle[vehicle_id]
    if not civilians.has(civilian_id):
        return
    var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
    var driver: ViceQuestCivilian = civilians[civilian_id]
    if not driver.in_vehicle:
        return
    var side: Vector3 = vehicle.get_side_vector()
    var eject_position: Vector3 = vehicle.position + side * 1.55
    eject_position.y = sample_surface_height(eject_position, vehicle.position.y - 0.10) + PLAYER_Y
    driver.eject_to(eject_position, side)
    driver.set_route(_get_nearest_sidewalk_route(eject_position))
    driver.trigger_panic(vehicle.position)
    civilian_stream_states[civilian_id] = true
    _sync_civilian_stream_state.rpc(civilian_id, true)
    _sync_civilian_state.rpc(civilian_id, driver.position, driver.facing, driver.is_alive, driver.hp, false, true)


--- lines 5676-5700 ---
func _server_enter_vehicle(player_id: int, vehicle_id: int) -> void:
    if not players.has(player_id) or not vehicles.has(vehicle_id):
        return
    var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
    if vehicle.driver_id != 0 or vehicle.is_destroyed:
        return
    var was_theft: bool = vehicle.claim_by_player()
    if was_theft:
        _eject_traffic_driver(vehicle_id)
        _add_wanted(player_id, 1, "VEHICLE THEFT")
    player_vehicle[player_id] = vehicle_id
    player_tuning_last_position[player_id] = vehicle.position
    vehicle.set_driver(player_id)
    inputs[player_id] = Vector2.ZERO
    var player: ViceQuestPlayer = players[player_id]
    player.snap_to_position(Vector3(vehicle.position.x, vehicle.position.y + 0.02, vehicle.position.z))
    player.facing = vehicle.get_forward_vector()
    player.set_vehicle_state(vehicle_id)
    _sync_vehicle_driver.rpc(vehicle_id, player_id)
    _play_vehicle_door.rpc(vehicle.position, _vehicle_uses_heavy_door_sound(vehicle), false)
    if was_theft:
        _show_combat_message.rpc("%s stole a civilian %s" % [_display_name(player_id), vehicle.display_name])
    else:
        _show_combat_message.rpc("%s entered a %s" % [_display_name(player_id), vehicle.display_name])


--- lines 5701-5728 ---
func _server_exit_vehicle(player_id: int) -> void:
    if not player_vehicle.has(player_id):
        return
    var vehicle_id: int = player_vehicle[player_id]
    if not vehicles.has(vehicle_id):
        player_vehicle.erase(player_id)
        return
    var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
    var side: Vector3 = vehicle.get_side_vector()
    var exit_position: Vector3 = vehicle.position + side * 1.28
    exit_position.y = sample_surface_height(exit_position, vehicle.position.y - 0.10) + PLAYER_Y
    exit_position.x = clampf(exit_position.x, PLAYABLE_MIN_X, WORLD_SIZE.x - 0.7)
    exit_position.z = clampf(exit_position.z, 0.7, WORLD_SIZE.y - 0.7)

    vehicle.set_driver(0)
    tank_turret_inputs.erase(player_id)
    vehicle.current_speed *= 0.72
    player_vehicle.erase(player_id)
    player_tuning_last_position.erase(player_id)
    if players.has(player_id):
        var player: ViceQuestPlayer = players[player_id]
        player.set_vehicle_state(0)
        player.snap_to_position(exit_position)
        player.facing = vehicle.get_forward_vector()
    _sync_vehicle_driver.rpc(vehicle_id, 0)
    _play_vehicle_door.rpc(vehicle.position, _vehicle_uses_heavy_door_sound(vehicle), true)
    _teleport_player.rpc(player_id, exit_position, vehicle.get_forward_vector())

--- lines 5748-5764 ---
func _sync_vehicle_driver(vehicle_id: int, driver_id: int) -> void:
    if not vehicles.has(vehicle_id):
        return
    var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
    var previous_driver: int = vehicle.driver_id
    if previous_driver != 0 and previous_driver != driver_id:
        player_vehicle.erase(previous_driver)
        if players.has(previous_driver):
            players[previous_driver].set_vehicle_state(0)
    vehicle.set_driver(driver_id)
    if driver_id != 0:
        player_vehicle[driver_id] = vehicle_id
        if players.has(driver_id):
            players[driver_id].set_vehicle_state(vehicle_id)
    _update_vehicle_ui()

--- lines 5807-5857 ---
func _apply_vehicle_runover_contacts(vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle, runover_speed: float) -> void:
    var motion_delta: Vector3 = vehicle.position - vehicle.last_motion_origin
    motion_delta.y = 0.0
    var impact_direction: Vector3 = motion_delta.normalized() if motion_delta.length_squared() > 0.0001 else vehicle.get_forward_vector() * (1.0 if vehicle.current_speed >= 0.0 else -1.0)
    var impact_strength: float = clampf(runover_speed * 0.82, 4.0, 13.5)
    var impact_attacker: int = vehicle.driver_id
    var center: Vector2i = _runover_cell(vehicle.position)
    for cell_x in range(center.x - 1, center.x + 2):
        for cell_y in range(center.y - 1, center.y + 2):
            var cell: Vector2i = Vector2i(cell_x, cell_y)
            if runover_civilian_grid.has(cell):
                var nearby_civilians: Array = runover_civilian_grid[cell]
                for raw_civilian_id: Variant in nearby_civilians:
                    var civilian_id: int = int(raw_civilian_id)
                    if not civilians.has(civilian_id):
                        continue
                    var civilian: ViceQuestCivilian = civilians[civilian_id]
                    if not civilian.is_alive or civilian.is_incapacitated() or not vehicle.is_runover_contact(civilian.position, 0.34):
                        continue
                    civilian.apply_knockback(impact_direction * impact_strength)
                    _damage_civilian(civilian_id, clampi(roundi(runover_speed * 5.2), 18, CIVILIAN_MAX_HP), impact_attacker)
                    if civilian.is_alive:
                        civilian.apply_unconscious(clampf(runover_speed * 0.32, 2.4, 5.0), vehicle.position)
                    _play_knockback_fx.rpc(civilian.position, impact_strength)
            if runover_cop_grid.has(cell):
                var nearby_cops: Array = runover_cop_grid[cell]
                for raw_cop_id: Variant in nearby_cops:
                    var cop_id: int = int(raw_cop_id)
                    if not cops.has(cop_id):
                        continue
                    var cop: ViceQuestCop = cops[cop_id]
                    if not cop.is_alive or cop.is_incapacitated() or not vehicle.is_runover_contact(cop.position, 0.32):
                        continue
                    cop.apply_knockback(impact_direction * impact_strength)
                    _damage_cop(cop_id, clampi(roundi(runover_speed * 4.4), 14, 65), impact_attacker)
                    if cop.is_alive:
                        cop.apply_unconscious(clampf(runover_speed * 0.30, 2.4, 4.8), vehicle.position)
                    _play_knockback_fx.rpc(cop.position, impact_strength)
            if runover_player_grid.has(cell):
                var nearby_players: Array = runover_player_grid[cell]
                for raw_player_id: Variant in nearby_players:
                    var player_id: int = int(raw_player_id)
                    if player_id == vehicle.driver_id or not alive_states.get(player_id, false):
                        continue
                    var hit_player: ViceQuestPlayer = players[player_id]
                    if not vehicle.is_runover_contact(hit_player.position, 0.36):
                        continue
                    hit_player.apply_knockback(impact_direction * impact_strength)
                    _damage_player(player_id, clampi(roundi(runover_speed * 3.4), 10, 55), impact_attacker)
                    _play_knockback_fx.rpc(hit_player.position, impact_strength)


--- lines 5858-5892 ---
func _broadcast_batched_world_snapshot() -> void:
    var player_rows: Array = []
    for raw_id: Variant in players.keys():
        var id: int = int(raw_id)
        var player: ViceQuestPlayer = players[id]
        var moving: bool = not player_vehicle.has(id) and inputs.has(id) and inputs[id].length_squared() > 0.0
        player_rows.append([id, player.position, player.facing, moving])
    var projectile_rows: Array = []
    for raw_projectile_id: Variant in active_projectiles.keys():
        var projectile_id: int = int(raw_projectile_id)
        projectile_rows.append([projectile_id, active_projectiles[projectile_id]["position"]])
    _sync_motion_snapshot.rpc(player_rows, projectile_rows)

    var vehicle_rows: Array = []
    for raw_vehicle_id: Variant in vehicles.keys():
        var vehicle_id: int = int(raw_vehicle_id)
        var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
        if vehicle.stream_active or vehicle.driver_id != 0:
            vehicle_rows.append([vehicle_id, vehicle.position, vehicle.heading, vehicle.current_speed, vehicle.ai_controlled, vehicle.turret_heading])
    _sync_vehicle_snapshot.rpc(vehicle_rows)

    var cop_rows: Array = []
    for raw_cop_id: Variant in cops.keys():
        var cop_id: int = int(raw_cop_id)
        var cop: ViceQuestCop = cops[cop_id]
        cop_rows.append([cop_id, cop.position, cop.facing, cop.is_alive, cop.hp, cop.knockdown_state()])
    var civilian_rows: Array = []
    for raw_civilian_id: Variant in civilians.keys():
        var civilian_id: int = int(raw_civilian_id)
        var civilian: ViceQuestCivilian = civilians[civilian_id]
        if civilian.stream_active:
            civilian_rows.append([civilian_id, civilian.position, civilian.facing, civilian.is_alive, civilian.hp, civilian.in_vehicle, civilian.panic_timer > 0.0, civilian.knockdown_state()])
    _sync_actor_snapshot.rpc(cop_rows, civilian_rows)
