--- lines 5926-6145 ---
func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
    if not game_started or not multiplayer.is_server():
        return

    for raw_cooldown_id in shot_cooldowns.keys():
        var cooldown_id: int = int(raw_cooldown_id)
        shot_cooldowns[cooldown_id] = maxf(0.0, shot_cooldowns[cooldown_id] - delta)
    for raw_reload_id in reload_timers.keys():
        var reload_id: int = int(raw_reload_id)
        var before: float = reload_timers[reload_id]
        reload_timers[reload_id] = maxf(0.0, before - delta)
        if before > 0.0 and reload_timers[reload_id] <= 0.0:
            _finish_reload(reload_id)
            _sync_ammo_state.rpc(reload_id, player_ammo_clip[reload_id], player_ammo_reserve[reload_id], 0.0)

    for raw_player_stun_id in player_stun_timers.keys():
        var player_stun_id: int = int(raw_player_stun_id)
        player_stun_timers[player_stun_id] = maxf(0.0, player_stun_timers[player_stun_id] - delta)
        if player_stun_timers[player_stun_id] <= 0.0:
            player_stun_timers.erase(player_stun_id)
    for raw_cop_stun_id in cop_stun_timers.keys():
        var cop_stun_id: int = int(raw_cop_stun_id)
        cop_stun_timers[cop_stun_id] = maxf(0.0, cop_stun_timers[cop_stun_id] - delta)
        if cop_stun_timers[cop_stun_id] <= 0.0:
            cop_stun_timers.erase(cop_stun_id)

    _server_projectile_step(delta)
    _server_fire_zone_step(delta)
    _server_burn_step(delta)
    _server_vehicle_gadget_step(delta)
    _server_update_pursuit_forces(delta)
    _server_mission_step(delta)
    service_poll_accumulator += delta
    if service_poll_accumulator >= SERVICE_POLL_INTERVAL:
        service_poll_accumulator = 0.0
        _server_gang_headquarters_step()
        _server_tuning_shop_step()

    var pickup_ready: Array[int] = []
    for raw_pickup_timer_id in ammo_pickup_timers.keys():
        var pickup_timer_id: int = int(raw_pickup_timer_id)
        ammo_pickup_timers[pickup_timer_id] = maxf(0.0, ammo_pickup_timers[pickup_timer_id] - delta)
        if ammo_pickup_timers[pickup_timer_id] <= 0.0:
            pickup_ready.append(pickup_timer_id)
    for pickup_id in pickup_ready:
        ammo_pickup_timers.erase(pickup_id)
        ammo_pickup_available[pickup_id] = true
        _sync_ammo_pickup_state.rpc(pickup_id, true)

    var weapon_pickup_ready: Array[int] = []
    for raw_weapon_pickup_timer_id in weapon_pickup_timers.keys():
        var weapon_pickup_timer_id: int = int(raw_weapon_pickup_timer_id)
        weapon_pickup_timers[weapon_pickup_timer_id] = maxf(0.0, weapon_pickup_timers[weapon_pickup_timer_id] - delta)
        if weapon_pickup_timers[weapon_pickup_timer_id] <= 0.0:
            weapon_pickup_ready.append(weapon_pickup_timer_id)
    for weapon_pickup_id in weapon_pickup_ready:
        weapon_pickup_timers.erase(weapon_pickup_id)
        weapon_pickup_available[weapon_pickup_id] = true
        _sync_weapon_pickup_state.rpc(weapon_pickup_id, true)

    for raw_wanted_id in wanted_levels.keys():
        var wanted_id: int = int(raw_wanted_id)
        var wanted_level: int = wanted_levels[wanted_id]
        if wanted_level <= 0:
            continue
        var wanted_timer: float = wanted_decay_timers[wanted_id] if wanted_decay_timers.has(wanted_id) else WANTED_DECAY_FIRST
        wanted_timer = maxf(0.0, wanted_timer - delta)
        wanted_decay_timers[wanted_id] = wanted_timer
        if wanted_timer <= 0.0:
            wanted_levels[wanted_id] = maxi(0, wanted_level - 1)
            wanted_decay_timers[wanted_id] = WANTED_DECAY_NEXT if wanted_levels[wanted_id] > 0 else 0.0
            _sync_wanted_level.rpc(wanted_id, wanted_levels[wanted_id])

    var ready_to_respawn: Array[int] = []
    for raw_respawn_id in respawn_timers.keys():
        var respawn_id: int = int(raw_respawn_id)
        respawn_timers[respawn_id] = maxf(0.0, respawn_timers[respawn_id] - delta)
        if respawn_timers[respawn_id] <= 0.0:
            ready_to_respawn.append(respawn_id)
    for respawn_id in ready_to_respawn:
        _respawn_player_server(respawn_id)

    var vehicles_ready_to_respawn: Array[int] = []
    for raw_vehicle_respawn_id in vehicle_respawn_timers.keys():
        var vehicle_respawn_id: int = int(raw_vehicle_respawn_id)
        vehicle_respawn_timers[vehicle_respawn_id] = maxf(0.0, vehicle_respawn_timers[vehicle_respawn_id] - delta)
        if vehicle_respawn_timers[vehicle_respawn_id] <= 0.0:
            vehicles_ready_to_respawn.append(vehicle_respawn_id)
    for vehicle_respawn_id in vehicles_ready_to_respawn:
        _respawn_vehicle_server(vehicle_respawn_id)

    var civilians_ready_to_respawn: Array[int] = []
    for raw_civilian_respawn_id in civilian_respawn_timers.keys():
        var civilian_respawn_id: int = int(raw_civilian_respawn_id)
        civilian_respawn_timers[civilian_respawn_id] = maxf(0.0, civilian_respawn_timers[civilian_respawn_id] - delta)
        if civilian_respawn_timers[civilian_respawn_id] <= 0.0:
            civilians_ready_to_respawn.append(civilian_respawn_id)
    for civilian_respawn_id in civilians_ready_to_respawn:
        _respawn_civilian_server(civilian_respawn_id)

    var cops_ready_to_respawn: Array[int] = []
    for raw_cop_respawn_id in cop_respawn_timers.keys():
        var cop_respawn_id: int = int(raw_cop_respawn_id)
        cop_respawn_timers[cop_respawn_id] = maxf(0.0, cop_respawn_timers[cop_respawn_id] - delta)
        if cop_respawn_timers[cop_respawn_id] <= 0.0:
            cops_ready_to_respawn.append(cop_respawn_id)
    for cop_respawn_id in cops_ready_to_respawn:
        cop_respawn_timers.erase(cop_respawn_id)
        if cops.has(cop_respawn_id):
            cops[cop_respawn_id].reset_to_spawn()
            var respawned_cop: ViceQuestCop = cops[cop_respawn_id]
            _sync_cop_state.rpc(cop_respawn_id, respawned_cop.position, respawned_cop.facing, respawned_cop.is_alive, respawned_cop.hp)

    population_stream_accumulator += delta
    if population_stream_accumulator >= POPULATION_STREAM_INTERVAL:
        population_stream_accumulator = 0.0
        _update_population_streaming()

    gang_decision_accumulator += delta
    var run_gang_decisions: bool = gang_decision_accumulator >= GANG_DECISION_INTERVAL
    var gang_decision_delta: float = gang_decision_accumulator
    if run_gang_decisions:

    for raw_cop_id in cops.keys():
        var cop_id: int = int(raw_cop_id)
        var cop: ViceQuestCop = cops[cop_id]

        if not cop.is_alive:
            continue
        cop.status_step(delta)
        if cop.is_incapacitated():

            cop.velocity = down_knockback
            if down_knockback.length_squared() > 0.04:
                cop.move_and_slide()
                cop.position.y = sample_surface_height(cop.position, cop.position.y - PLAYER_Y) + PLAYER_Y
            continue
        var burning_flee: Vector3 = cop.take_burning_step(delta)
        if burning_flee.length_squared() > 0.04:
            cop.velocity = burning_flee
            cop.rotation.y = -atan2(cop.facing.x, -cop.facing.z)
            cop.set_motion_state(true, false)

            cop.position.y = sample_surface_height(cop.position, cop.position.y - PLAYER_Y) + PLAYER_Y
            continue
        var cop_knockback: Vector3 = cop.take_knockback_step(delta)
        if cop_knockback.length_squared() > 0.04:
            cop.velocity = cop_knockback
            cop.facing = cop_knockback.normalized()
            cop.rotation.y = -atan2(cop.facing.x, -cop.facing.z)
            cop.set_motion_state(true, false)
            cop.move_and_slide()
            cop.position.y = sample_surface_height(cop.position, cop.position.y - PLAYER_Y) + PLAYER_Y

            if dist < best_distance:

                cop.rotation.y = -atan2(cop.facing.x, -cop.facing.z)

func _update_vehicle_ui() -> void:
    if vehicle_status_label == null:
        return
    var local_id: int = multiplayer.get_unique_id()
    if player_vehicle.has(local_id):
        var vehicle_id: int = player_vehicle[local_id]
        if vehicles.has(vehicle_id):
            var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
            var mods: Array[String] = []
            if vehicle.has_vehicle_gun:
                mods.append("GUN")
            if vehicle.has_mines:
                mods.append("MINES")
            if vehicle.has_oil_slick:
                mods.append("OIL")
            var mod_text: String = "+".join(mods) if not mods.is_empty() else "STOCK"
            var shop: Dictionary = TUNING_DATA.shop_near(vehicle.position)
            var controls: String = input_bridge.control_hint(false, vehicle.variant_id == "tank") if input_bridge != null else ("E tune" if not shop.is_empty() else "E exit")
            if vehicle.variant_id == "tank":
                vehicle_status_label.text = "TANK #%d // %d/%d HP // %.1f/%.1f\n%s" % [vehicle_id, vehicle.hp, vehicle.max_hp, absf(vehicle.current_speed), vehicle.max_forward_speed, controls]
            else:
                vehicle_status_label.text = "%s #%d // %d/%d HP // %.1f/%.1f // %s\n%s" % [vehicle.display_name, vehicle_id, vehicle.hp, vehicle.max_hp, absf(vehicle.current_speed), vehicle.max_forward_speed, mod_text, controls]
            if tuning_panel != null and tuning_panel.visible and (tuning_vehicle_id != vehicle_id or shop.is_empty()):
                _close_tuning_menu()
            return
    if tuning_panel != null and tuning_panel.visible:
        _close_tuning_menu()
    vehicle_status_label.text = input_bridge.control_hint(true, false) if input_bridge != null else "ON FOOT // E interact • R reload"

func _vehicle_spawn_is_clear(candidate: Vector3, minimum_distance: float = 5.2) -> bool:
    for raw_vehicle_id in vehicles.keys():
        var vehicle_id: int = int(raw_vehicle_id)
        var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
        var active: bool = vehicle.driver_id != 0 or (vehicle_stream_states.has(vehicle_id) and vehicle_stream_states[vehicle_id])
        if active and not vehicle.is_destroyed and _planar_distance(candidate, vehicle.position) < minimum_distance:
            return false
    return true

func _ped_spawn_is_clear(candidate: Vector3, minimum_distance: float = 1.35) -> bool:
    for raw_civilian_id in civilians.keys():
        var civilian_id: int = int(raw_civilian_id)
        var civilian: ViceQuestCivilian = civilians[civilian_id]
        if civilian_stream_states.has(civilian_id) and civilian_stream_states[civilian_id] and civilian.is_alive and not civilian.in_vehicle:
            if _planar_distance(candidate, civilian.position) < minimum_distance:
                return false
    return true

func _configure_civilian_gang(civilian: ViceQuestCivilian, world_position: Vector3) -> void:
    var territory_gang: int = GANG_DATA.downtown_gang_at(world_position)
    var stable_key: int = civilian.civilian_id * 37 + roundi(world_position.x) * 3 + roundi(world_position.z)

    var assigned_gang: int = territory_gang if territory_gang > 0 and posmod(stable_key, 5) < 3 else GANG_DATA.NEUTRAL
    civilian.configure_gang(assigned_gang)

func _request_ambient_vehicle_recycle(vehicle_id: int) -> void:
    if not multiplayer.is_server() or not ambient_vehicle_ids.has(vehicle_id) or not vehicles.has(vehicle_id):
        return
    var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
    if vehicle.driver_id != 0 or not vehicle.ai_controlled:
        return
    if _try_continue_ambient_vehicle_route(vehicle_id):
        return
    # Never recycle a car while it is still in the player's local presentation window.
    if _is_near_any_player(vehicle.position, TRAFFIC_ROUTE_END_MIN_RADIUS):
        return
    _deactivate_ambient_vehicle(vehicle_id)

func _poll_internet_port_mapping() -> void:
    if upnp_thread == null or upnp_thread.is_alive():
        return
    var result_variant: Variant = upnp_thread.wait_to_finish()
    upnp_thread = null
    if not result_variant is Dictionary:
        if network_status_label != null:
            network_status_label.text = "INTERNET // UPnP returned no result. Forward UDP %d manually." % PORT
        return
    var result: Dictionary = result_variant
    var gateway_variant: Variant = result.get("gateway")

func _melee_candidate_score(origin: Vector3, direction: Vector3, target: Vector3, weapon_range: float) -> float:
    var to_target: Vector3 = target - origin
    var vertical_distance: float = absf(to_target.y)

@rpc("authority", "call_local", "unreliable")

func _panic_civilians(origin: Vector3, radius: float = 9.0) -> void:
    for raw_civilian_id in civilians.keys():
        var civilian_id: int = int(raw_civilian_id)
        var civilian: ViceQuestCivilian = civilians[civilian_id]
        if civilian.stream_active and civilian.is_alive and not civilian.in_vehicle and origin.distance_to(civilian.position) <= radius:
            civilian.trigger_panic(origin)
    _panic_traffic(origin, radius * 1.15)

... context 4142 ...
        # quarter abandon the car and use the existing pedestrian panic AI.
        var reaction_key: int = posmod(vehicle_id * 31 + roundi(origin.x) * 7 + roundi(origin.z), 4)
        if reaction_key == 0:
            vehicle.ai_controlled = false
            vehicle.current_speed = 0.0
            _eject_traffic_driver(vehicle_id)
        else:
            vehicle.trigger_panic(6.0 + float(reaction_key))

func _damage_player(target_id: int, amount: int, attacker_id: int, blood: bool = true) -> void:
    if not healths.has(target_id) or not alive_states.has(target_id) or not alive_states[target_id]:
        return
    var new_hp: int = maxi(0, healths[target_id] - amount)
    if blood and players.has(target_id):
        var hit_direction: Vector3 = players[target_id].position - players[attacker_id].position if players.has(attacker_id) else players[target_id].facing
        _play_blood_fx.rpc(players[target_id].position, hit_direction, new_hp <= 0)
    healths[target_id] = new_hp
    if new_hp <= 0:
        _kill_player(target_id, attacker_id)
    else:
        _sync_health_state.rpc(target_id, new_hp, true)

... context 5221 ...
func _update_combat_ui() -> void:
    if health_label == null or health_bar == null:
        return
    var local_id: int = multiplayer.get_unique_id()
    var current_hp: int = healths[local_id] if healths.has(local_id) else MAX_HP
    var alive: bool = alive_states[local_id] if alive_states.has(local_id) else true
    health_bar.value = float(current_hp)
    health_label.text = "HP %d / %d" % [current_hp, MAX_HP]
    if not alive:
        health_label.text = "WASTED // respawning..."
    _update_weapon_ui()

func _server_open_tuning_shop(player_id: int, vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle, shop: Dictionary) -> bool:
    if shop.is_empty() or vehicle == null or vehicle.driver_id != player_id or vehicle.is_destroyed:
        return false
    var shop_id: String = String(shop["id"])
    var already_waiting: bool = (
        String(player_tuning_shop_inside.get(player_id, "")) == shop_id
        and bool(player_tuning_paused.get(player_id, false))
    )
    vehicle.current_speed = 0.0
    inputs[player_id] = Vector2.ZERO
    player_tuning_shop_inside[player_id] = shop_id

... context 5487 ...
    var already_waiting: bool = (
        String(player_tuning_shop_inside.get(player_id, "")) == shop_id
        and bool(player_tuning_paused.get(player_id, false))
    )
    vehicle.current_speed = 0.0
    inputs[player_id] = Vector2.ZERO
    player_tuning_shop_inside[player_id] = shop_id
    player_tuning_paused[player_id] = true
    if not already_waiting:
        _send_tuning_menu(player_id, vehicle, String(shop["name"]), true)
    return true

func _server_enter_vehicle(player_id: int, vehicle_id: int) -> void:
    if not players.has(player_id) or not vehicles.has(vehicle_id):
        return
    var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
    if vehicle.driver_id != 0 or vehicle.is_destroyed:
        return
    var was_theft: bool = vehicle.claim_by_player()
    if was_theft:
        _eject_traffic_driver(vehicle_id)
        _add_wanted(player_id, 1, "VEHICLE THEFT")
    player_vehicle[player_id] = vehicle_id
    player_tuning_last_position[player_id] = vehicle.position
    vehicle.set_driver(player_id)
    inputs[player_id] = Vector2.ZERO

@rpc("authority", "call_local", "reliable")
func _sync_vehicle_driver(vehicle_id: int, driver_id: int) -> void:
    if not vehicles.has(vehicle_id):
        return
    var vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle = vehicles[vehicle_id]
    var previous_driver: int = vehicle.driver_id
    if previous_driver != 0 and previous_driver != driver_id:
        player_vehicle.erase(previous_driver)
        if players.has(previous_driver):
            players[previous_driver].set_vehicle_state(0)
    vehicle.set_driver(driver_id)

func _planar_distance(a: Vector3, b: Vector3) -> float:
    return Vector2(a.x, a.z).distance_to(Vector2(b.x, b.z))

func _damage_texture_path() -> String:
    if is_destroyed:
        var wreck_index: int = int(WRECK_BY_VARIANT.get(variant_id, 3))
        return "res://assets/vehicles/wrecks/wreck_%d.png" % wreck_index
    return "res://assets/vehicles/%s.png" % variant_id


--- lines 336-345 ---
func _build_collision() -> void:
    if _collision != null:
        return
    _collision = CollisionShape3D.new()
    var shape: BoxShape3D = BoxShape3D.new()
    shape.size = Vector3(1.72, 0.52, 0.78)
    _collision.shape = shape
    _collision.position.y = 0.28
    add_child(_collision)

func set_driver(new_driver_id: int) -> void:
    driver_id = new_driver_id
    if driver_id != 0:

        _ensure_runtime_resources()

--- lines 489-502 ---
func set_damage_state(new_hp: int, destroyed: bool) -> void:
    var previous_hp: int = hp
    var was_destroyed: bool = is_destroyed
    hp = clampi(new_hp, 0, max_hp)
    is_destroyed = destroyed
    if is_destroyed:
        driver_id = 0
        current_speed = 0.0
        velocity = Vector3.ZERO
    _apply_damage_visuals()
    if _sprite != null and (hp < previous_hp or (is_destroyed and not was_destroyed)):
        _play_damage_response(is_destroyed)
    _refresh_label()


--- lines 503-542 ---
func _apply_damage_visuals() -> void:
    # Streaming sleeps AI/physics. Visibility stays independent so a bad stream
    # decision can never turn Downtown into an empty city again.
    var damaged_threshold_hp: int = roundi(float(max_hp) * 0.65)
    var smoke_threshold_hp: int = roundi(float(max_hp) * 0.35)
    damage_stage = 3 if is_destroyed else (2 if hp <= smoke_threshold_hp else (1 if hp <= damaged_threshold_hp else 0))
    var physically_present: bool = stream_active and not crushed_by_tank
    if _sprite != null:
        _sprite.visible = stream_active or not ambient_pool_member
        var texture_path: String = _damage_texture_path()
        if ResourceLoader.exists(texture_path):
            _sprite.texture = _vehicle_texture(texture_path)
        var damage_tint: Color = Color.WHITE
        match damage_stage:
            1:
                damage_tint = Color(0.82, 0.78, 0.70, 1.0)
            2:
                damage_tint = Color(0.58, 0.52, 0.46, 1.0)
        _sprite.modulate = Color(
            paint_color.r * damage_tint.r,
            paint_color.g * damage_tint.g,
            paint_color.b * damage_tint.b,
            1.0
        )
        _sprite.position.y = 0.025 if is_destroyed else 0.05
    _configure_variant_size(is_destroyed)
    if _collision != null:
        _collision.set_deferred("disabled", not physically_present)
    collision_layer = 1 if physically_present else 0
    collision_mask = 1 if physically_present else 0
    if physically_present and hp > 0 and hp <= smoke_threshold_hp:
        _ensure_smoke()
    if _smoke != null:
        _smoke.emitting = physically_present and hp > 0 and hp <= smoke_threshold_hp
        if hp <= 35 and hp > 0:
            _smoke.amount = 16
        else:
            _smoke.amount = 10
    _refresh_tank_turret()


--- lines 543-551 ---
func _play_damage_response(wreck: bool) -> void:
    if _sprite == null:
        return
    if _damage_tween != null and _damage_tween.is_valid():
        _damage_tween.kill()
    _sprite.scale = Vector3(1.12, 0.88, 1.0) if wreck else Vector3(1.06, 0.94, 1.0)
    _damage_tween = create_tween()
    _damage_tween.tween_property(_sprite, "scale", Vector3.ONE, 0.18 if wreck else 0.10)


--- lines 638-642 ---
func consume_crash_damage() -> int:
    var value: int = _pending_crash_damage
    _pending_crash_damage = 0
    return value

        return
    pursuit_active = true
    pursuit_target = world_position
    pursuit_role = role
    ai_controlled = true
    stream_active = true

--- lines 686-720 ---
func _server_pursuit_step(delta: float) -> void:
    var to_target: Vector3 = pursuit_target - position
    to_target.y = 0.0
    if to_target.length_squared() <= 0.04:
        current_speed = move_toward(current_speed, 0.0, brake_deceleration * delta)
        velocity = _take_external_impulse_step(delta)
        move_and_slide()
        return
    var desired_direction: Vector3 = to_target.normalized()
    var desired_heading: float = atan2(desired_direction.z, desired_direction.x)
    var turn_speed: float = STEER_RATE * (1.15 if pursuit_role == "police" else 0.92)
    heading = lerp_angle(heading, desired_heading, clampf(turn_speed * delta, 0.0, 1.0))
    rotation.y = -heading
    var forward: Vector3 = get_forward_vector()
    var alignment: float = clampf(forward.dot(desired_direction), -0.4, 1.0)
    var chase_speed: float = max_forward_speed * (0.96 if pursuit_role == "police" else 0.90)
    if alignment < 0.25:
        chase_speed *= 0.42
    current_speed = move_toward(current_speed, chase_speed, acceleration * delta)
    var impact_speed: float = absf(current_speed)
    last_impact_speed = impact_speed
    var previous_position: Vector3 = position
    velocity = forward * current_speed + _take_external_impulse_step(delta)
    move_and_slide()
    if _enforce_vehicle_surface(previous_position):
        _snap_to_map_surface()
    if get_slide_collision_count() > 0:
        if impact_speed >= CRASH_DAMAGE_MIN_SPEED and _crash_damage_cooldown <= 0.0:
            _pending_crash_damage = clampi(roundi((impact_speed - 6.5) * 3.2), 4, 22)
            _crash_damage_cooldown = CRASH_DAMAGE_COOLDOWN
        current_speed *= -0.18
        _resolve_vehicle_collisions(impact_speed)
    target_position = position
    target_heading = heading


--- lines 721-733 ---
func _enforce_vehicle_surface(previous_position: Vector3) -> bool:
    var main_node: Node = get_parent()
    if main_node == null or not main_node.has_method("is_vehicle_surface_allowed"):
        return true
    if bool(main_node.call("is_vehicle_surface_allowed", previous_position, position)):
        return true
    position = previous_position
    velocity = Vector3.ZERO
    external_impulse_velocity = Vector3.ZERO
    current_speed *= -0.16
    target_position = position
    return false


--- lines 734-778 ---
func _resolve_vehicle_collisions(impact_speed: float) -> void:
    for collision_index in range(get_slide_collision_count()):
        var collision: KinematicCollision3D = get_slide_collision(collision_index)
        var collider: Object = collision.get_collider()
        if not collider is ViceQuestVehicle:
            continue
        var other: ViceQuestVehicle = collider as ViceQuestVehicle
        if other == self or other.is_destroyed:
            continue
        if variant_id == "tank" and other.variant_id != "tank" and multiplayer.is_server():
            var main_node: Node = get_parent()
            if main_node != null and main_node.has_method("_server_tank_crush_vehicle"):
                main_node.call("_server_tank_crush_vehicle", vehicle_id, other.vehicle_id, driver_id)
            # The victim becomes non-collidable immediately. Preserve most tank
            # momentum and advance through the old contact plane so the tank
            # behaves as a heavy tracked vehicle instead of bouncing backward.
            var travel_sign: float = 1.0 if current_speed >= 0.0 else -1.0
            current_speed = travel_sign * maxf(absf(current_speed), impact_speed * 0.82)
            position += get_forward_vector() * travel_sign * clampf(impact_speed * 0.045, 0.38, 0.92)
            _snap_to_map_surface()
            target_position = position
            continue
        var normal: Vector3 = collision.get_normal()
        normal.y = 0.0
        if normal.length_squared() <= 0.001:
            normal = (position - other.position).normalized()
        else:
            normal = normal.normalized()
        var relative_speed: float = maxf(impact_speed, (velocity - other.velocity).length())
        if relative_speed < 1.2:
            continue
        var self_mass: float = clampf(collision_mass / 16.0, 0.35, 2.2)
        var other_mass: float = clampf(other.collision_mass / 16.0, 0.35, 2.2)
        var impulse: float = clampf(relative_speed * 0.48, 1.4, 9.5)
        apply_external_impulse(normal * impulse * other_mass / self_mass)
        other.apply_external_impulse(-normal * impulse * self_mass / other_mass)
        current_speed *= -0.22
        other.current_speed *= 0.55
        if _crash_damage_cooldown <= 0.0 and relative_speed >= CRASH_DAMAGE_MIN_SPEED:
            _pending_crash_damage = maxi(_pending_crash_damage, clampi(roundi(relative_speed * 1.6), 5, 24))
            _crash_damage_cooldown = CRASH_DAMAGE_COOLDOWN
        if other._crash_damage_cooldown <= 0.0 and relative_speed >= CRASH_DAMAGE_MIN_SPEED:
            other._pending_crash_damage = maxi(other._pending_crash_damage, clampi(roundi(relative_speed * 1.35), 4, 20))
            other._crash_damage_cooldown = CRASH_DAMAGE_COOLDOWN

func server_turret_step(delta: float, input_axis: float) -> void:

func apply_oil_slip(stable_seed: int) -> void:
    if is_destroyed or absf(current_speed) < 2.0:
        return
    var side_sign: float = -1.0 if (stable_seed & 1) == 0 else 1.0
    heading = wrapf(heading + side_sign * clampf(absf(current_speed) * 0.035, 0.16, 0.46), -PI, PI)
    current_speed *= 0.78
    apply_external_impulse(get_side_vector() * side_sign * minf(4.2, absf(current_speed) * 0.36))
    target_heading = heading

func _refresh_visuals() -> void:

... context 198 ...

func _refresh_visuals() -> void:
    # Keep the body visible after death so the death sequence can finish.
    visible = true
    var physically_present: bool = is_alive and not is_incapacitated()
    collision_layer = 1 if physically_present else 0
    collision_mask = 1 if physically_present else 0
    if _collision != null:
        _collision.set_deferred("disabled", not physically_present)
    if _label != null:
        _label.visible = is_alive
    _update_animation()

===== FILE scripts/audio_manager.gd =====

--- lines 70-79 ---
func _stream(sample_key: String) -> AudioStream:
    if _cache.has(sample_key):
        return _cache[sample_key] as AudioStream
    var path: String = SFX_ROOT + sample_key + ".wav"
    if not ResourceLoader.exists(path):
        return null
    var stream: AudioStream = load(path) as AudioStream
    _cache[sample_key] = stream
    return stream


--- lines 80-89 ---
func _looped_stream(sample_key: String) -> AudioStream:
    var base: AudioStream = _stream(sample_key)
    if base == null:
        return null
    if base is AudioStreamWAV:
        var looped: AudioStreamWAV = (base as AudioStreamWAV).duplicate() as AudioStreamWAV
        looped.loop_mode = AudioStreamWAV.LOOP_FORWARD
        return looped
    return base

func play_vehicle_impact(world_position: Vector3, heavy: bool = false) -> void:
    _play_spatial("impact_heavy" if heavy else "impact_light", world_position, -8.0, 0.92, 24.0)


--- lines 114-121 ---
func play_vehicle_door(world_position: Vector3, heavy: bool, opening: bool) -> void:
    var key: String
    if heavy:
        key = "door_heavy_open" if opening else "door_heavy_close"
    else:
        key = "door_light_open" if opening else "door_light_close"
    _play_spatial(key, world_position, -7.0, 1.0, 18.0)


--- lines 122-142 ---
func update_local_vehicle(vehicle: ViceQuestVehicle) -> void:
    if engine_player == null:
        return
    if vehicle == null or vehicle.is_destroyed or vehicle.driver_id == 0:
        stop_engine()
        return
    var key: String = String(ENGINE_BY_VARIANT.get(vehicle.variant_id, "engine_standard"))
    if key != _engine_key or engine_player.stream == null:
        _engine_key = key
        engine_player.stream = _looped_stream(key)
        if engine_player.stream != null:
            engine_player.play()
    if engine_player.stream == null:
        return
    engine_player.global_position = vehicle.global_position
    var speed_ratio: float = clampf(absf(vehicle.current_speed) / maxf(vehicle.max_forward_speed, 0.1), 0.0, 1.0)
    engine_player.pitch_scale = 0.76 + speed_ratio * (0.48 if vehicle.variant_id == "tank" else 0.64)
    engine_player.volume_db = lerpf(-13.0, -5.0, speed_ratio)
    if not engine_player.playing:
        engine_player.play()


--- lines 143-147 ---
func stop_engine() -> void:
    _engine_key = ""
    if engine_player != null and engine_player.playing:
        engine_player.stop()

func _play_spatial(sample_key: String, world_position: Vector3, volume_db: float, pitch: float, max_distance: float) -> void:
    var stream: AudioStream = _stream(sample_key)
    if stream == null or _oneshot_pool.is_empty():
        return
    var player: AudioStreamPlayer3D = _oneshot_pool[_oneshot_cursor]
    _oneshot_cursor = (_oneshot_cursor + 1) % _oneshot_pool.size()
    player.stop()
    player.stream = stream

func _build_collision() -> void:
    _collision = CollisionShape3D.new()
    var shape: CapsuleShape3D = CapsuleShape3D.new()
    shape.radius = 0.34
    shape.height = PLAYER_HEIGHT
    _collision.shape = shape
    _collision.position.y = PLAYER_HEIGHT * 0.5
    add_child(_collision)

func set_vehicle_state(new_vehicle_id: int) -> void:
    vehicle_id = new_vehicle_id
    var in_vehicle: bool = vehicle_id != 0
    if _collision != null:
        _collision.set_deferred("disabled", in_vehicle or not is_alive)
    if _ped_visual != null:
        _ped_visual.visible = not in_vehicle
        _ped_visual.set_animation_enabled(not in_vehicle)
        if not in_vehicle and is_alive:
            _ped_visual.play_state("idle", true)
    if _shadow != null:

    _refresh_labels()

        _fire_pose_remaining = 0.0

func _physics_process(_delta: float) -> void:
    if not is_local or not is_alive:
        return

    var main_node: Node = get_parent()
    var dir: Vector2 = main_node.call("local_movement_input")
    main_node.call("submit_local_input", peer_id, dir)

    var interact_down: bool = bool(main_node.call("local_interact_down"))
    if interact_down and not _interact_was_down:
        var interaction_consumed: bool = bool(main_node.call("consume_local_interaction_press", peer_id))
        if not interaction_consumed:
            main_node.call("submit_interaction", peer_id)
    _interact_was_down = interact_down

    if vehicle_id != 0:
        set_moving_state(false)
        var turret_axis: float = float(main_node.call("local_turret_axis"))
        main_node.call("submit_tank_turret", peer_id, turret_axis)
        var fire_down: bool = bool(main_node.call("local_fire_down"))
        var mine_down: bool = bool(main_node.call("local_vehicle_mine_down"))
        var oil_down: bool = bool(main_node.call("local_vehicle_oil_down"))
        var tuning_open: bool = bool(main_node.call("is_tuning_menu_open_for_local"))
        if not tuning_open:
            if fire_down and not _fire_was_down:
                main_node.call("submit_fire", peer_id)
            if mine_down and not _reload_was_down:
                main_node.call("submit_vehicle_gadget", peer_id, "mine")
            if oil_down and not _gadget_was_down:
                main_node.call("submit_vehicle_gadget", peer_id, "oil")
        _fire_was_down = fire_down
        _reload_was_down = mine_down
        _gadget_was_down = oil_down
        return

    _poll_weapon_slots(main_node)
    var weapon_prev_down: bool = bool(main_node.call("local_weapon_previous_down"))
    var weapon_next_down: bool = bool(main_node.call("local_weapon_next_down"))
    if weapon_prev_down and not _weapon_prev_was_down:
        main_node.call("submit_weapon_cycle", peer_id, -1)
    if weapon_next_down and not _weapon_next_was_down:
        main_node.call("submit_weapon_cycle", peer_id, 1)
    _weapon_prev_was_down = weapon_prev_down
    _weapon_next_was_down = weapon_next_down
    set_moving_state(dir.length_squared() > 0.0)
    if dir.length_squared() > 0.0:
        facing = Vector3(dir.x, 0.0, dir.y).normalized()

    var fire_down: bool = bool(main_node.call("local_fire_down"))
    var automatic_fire: bool = bool(main_node.call("is_weapon_automatic_for", peer_id))
    if fire_down and (automatic_fire or not _fire_was_down):
        main_node.call("submit_fire", peer_id)
    _fire_was_down = fire_down

    var reload_down: bool = bool(main_node.call("local_reload_down"))
    if reload_down and not _reload_was_down:
        main_node.call("submit_reload", peer_id)
    _reload_was_down = reload_down
    _gadget_was_down = bool(main_node.call("local_vehicle_oil_down"))

func apply_knockback(impulse: Vector3) -> void:
    impulse.y = 0.0
    knockback_velocity = (knockback_velocity + impulse).limit_length(MAX_KNOCKBACK_SPEED)

func take_knockback_step(delta: float) -> Vector3:
    var result: Vector3 = knockback_velocity
    knockback_velocity = knockback_velocity.move_toward(Vector3.ZERO, KNOCKBACK_DECELERATION * delta)
    return result
